## [HN-TITLE] 16. Muse Glimmer: 30B-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows

- **Source**: [https://research.meta.ai/blog/introducing-muse-glimmer-open-agentic-model](https://research.meta.ai/blog/introducing-muse-glimmer-open-agentic-model)
- **Site**: Meta AI
- **Author**: Meta Superintelligence Labs
- **Published**: 2026-08-10
- **HN activity**: 1058 points · [585 comments](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241679)
- **Length**: 1.2K words (~6 min read)
- **Language**: en

Today, we're introducing Muse Glimmer, the next model from Meta Superintelligence Labs, and open sourcing the model weights under a permissive Apache 2.0 license.

Muse Glimmer is a 30-billion-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows. It’s small enough to run on a Mac or PC with a single consumer GPU, enabling use cases that range from local agents and function calling, to local coding, and LLM-as-a-judge evaluation. Muse Glimmer delivers strong performance on key agentic use cases and benchmarks compared with leading models in its size category.

Foundation models have achieved remarkable capabilities across reasoning, code generation, and tool use — yet most deployments still depend on cloud infrastructure and network access. Running models locally enables you to use AI anywhere, anytime, with or without an internet connection. This is increasingly viable: the open source community has shown that smaller models, when trained effectively, can approach frontier-level performance on targeted tasks. Muse Glimmer is optimized for these local use cases.

Keeping with our long tradition of sharing fundamental AI research, we're releasing Muse Glimmer open weights today on [Hugging Face](https://huggingface.co/meta-models/Muse-Glimmer-30B), along with [developer documentation](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/muse-glimmer) to help you start building and running your own agents. Muse Glimmer is built to work with the tools developers already use. Optimized integrations on llama.cpp, MLX, and ExecuTorch will land in the coming days, so you can go from download to working agent in minutes.

## How We Trained Muse Glimmer

An agent that manages your schedule, drafts your messages, organizes your files, and learns how you work needs deep access to personal context. It also needs several capabilities working in concert: long-horizon execution, precise tool calling, multimodal understanding, long-context memory, and instruction following.

We designed Muse Glimmer to balance capability against the memory and compute constraints of local hardware. This required a compact architecture, a novel distillation recipe that transfers agentic reasoning from a much larger teacher model, and inference optimizations — including quantization — to meet latency expectations. We achieved this in the following phases:

- **Pre-Training.** We trained Muse Glimmer on Muse Spark's outputs using logit distillation, leveraging a similar data mix as the teacher.
- **Mid-Training.** We trained the model on longer-context, more agent-heavy data with richer reasoning traces, alongside organic data.
- **Post-Training.** We combined supervised fine-tuning with a mix of on-policy distillation and reinforcement learning across general, reasoning, coding, and agentic domains.

Muse Glimmer was evaluated under the standards set out in [Meta's Advanced AI Scaling Framework](https://ai.meta.com/blog/scaling-how-we-build-test-advanced-ai/) and assessed for open-weight release across all relevant categories.

## Built for Agents: What Muse Glimmer Can Do

Building effective agents requires key capabilities working together to achieve the user’s goals. Muse Glimmer is trained and evaluated across each of the following:

- **End-to-end Agentic Task Completion.** Muse Glimmer achieves strong success rates on full-task benchmarks including DeepSearch QA, MCP-Atlas, 𝛕-Bench and SWE-Bench, which measure its ability to work within scaffolds, write and debug code, and resolve multi-turn requests from start to finish.
- **Reliable Tool Use.** The model handles a wide range of function calls, invoking tools with precise schemas throughout extended workflows.
- **Multi-Step Reasoning.** Muse Glimmer chains reasoning over long horizons, sustaining coherent plans across complex, extended workflows.
- **Failure Recovery.** When a tool call fails or returns an unexpected result, the model is trained to diagnose the error and retry rather than halt.
- **Multimodal Input and Reasoning.** Through a dedicated perception encoder, the model accepts interleaved text and images. This enables agents to interpret screenshots, charts, and documents alongside conversation.
- **Scaffold Compatibility.** Muse Glimmer works across OpenClaw and other agentic orchestration patterns.
- **Controllable Effort.** Muse Glimmer supports different reasoning strengths to select the right balance between quality and speed.
- **Multilingual.** Muse Glimmer is trained on data from more than 100 languages.

## Performance

We evaluated Muse Glimmer across a broad range of benchmarks to assess the diverse capabilities required for effective autonomous agent behavior. Compared with Gemma4-31B and Qwen3.6-27B, Muse Glimmer performs strongly for its size class on several widely used LLM benchmarks.

![Table comparing Muse Glimmer 30B with Gemma4 31B and Qwen3.6 27B across agentic, coding, multimodal, safety, and reasoning benchmarks.](https://lookaside.fbsbx.com/elementpath/media/?media_id=1763817931416938&version=1786388559)

For more detail about our evaluations, see [our report](https://research.meta.ai/static/muse-glimmer-methodology).

## Optimized for Local Deployments

A local agent is truly useful if it's fast enough to feel responsive. An agent that takes minutes to reply or plan its next step breaks the flow of real work. We applied two optimizations to make Muse Glimmer run at practical speeds on consumer hardware without sacrificing quality.

### Fitting the Model on Your Device.

At full precision, a 30-billion parameter model would require over 55 GB of memory — far more than any consumer GPU offers. We use quantization techniques to compress the model's weights to approximately 4-bit precision, shrinking the language model to under 20 GB. This leaves enough headroom for the model's working memory (its "KV cache"), the perception encoder for image understanding, and the speculative decoding drafter to run simultaneously within a 24 GB or 32 GB envelope. We validated that this compression introduces minimal to no degradation on agentic tasks.

![Table comparing full precision, K-Quant-Dynamic, and K-Quant-17GB by accuracy degradation and target hardware memory.](https://lookaside.fbsbx.com/elementpath/media/?media_id=1479406130664613&version=1786319248)

### Faster Generation Through Speculative Decoding.

Language models normally generate text one token at a time, which can feel slow during long reasoning chains or multi-step tool calls. Muse Glimmer ships with a lightweight "drafter" model based on [DFlash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06036) — a small companion network that proposes entire blocks of tokens at once. The main model then verifies these proposals in parallel, accepting correct tokens and correcting wrong ones. This technique lets Muse Glimmer generate text significantly faster than standard token-by-token generation while producing identical output quality. We provide quantized drafter versions to incur a smaller memory overhead in the release.

### The Result:

We measure the speed of our K-Quant-17GB model alongside the quantized DFlash drafter on MacBook M4-Max, M5-Max and on a RTX-5090. The model is fast enough for fluid conversation and real-time agent interaction, all running entirely on your device.

![Bar chart showing DFlash speculative decoding increasing Muse Glimmer decode speed by 3.1 times on RTX 5090, 1.8 times on M5 Max, and 1.5 times on M4 Max.](https://lookaside.fbsbx.com/elementpath/media/?media_id=1786825739136571&version=1786319248)

## Get Started With Muse Glimmer Today

Muse Glimmer is available now, and you can download the weights on [Hugging Face](https://huggingface.co/meta-models/Muse-Glimmer-30B). In the coming days, run it locally through partners like Ollama, LM Studio, and Unsloth, deploy it with edge frameworks including llama.cpp, ExecuTorch, and MLX, serve it at scale with vLLM and SGLang, or get started quickly through partners like Together AI, Fireworks AI, and OpenRouter. You can even customize it for your use case by leveraging PyTorch’s TorchTitan training feature to tune the model further.

We're also working with our partners including AMD, Arm, Dell, Intel, and NVIDIA to optimize performance across devices. In addition, we’re releasing [documentation](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/muse-glimmer) so developers have the resources they need to get started and build responsibly with Muse Glimmer. This includes guidance on setting up custom scaffolds, so it's even easier to start building and deploying personal agents on day one. You can learn more and find resources to build on [Meta's AI Developer Center](https://developer.meta.com/ai/models/muse-glimmer/).

This work builds on Meta's long track record of open AI research, extending it into agentic AI and giving developers access to local agentic capabilities. As always, we welcome feedback from the community and can’t wait to see what developers build with this open weights model.

[Download the Model on Hugging Face](https://huggingface.co/meta-models/Muse-Glimmer-30B) [Developer Documentation](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/muse-glimmer)

## [HN-TITLE] 17. Squeak 6.1

- **Source**: [https://squeak.org/release\_notes/6.1/](https://squeak.org/release_notes/6.1/)
- **Site**: Squeak.org
- **Author**: Squeak.org
- **Published**: 2026-08-10
- **HN activity**: 234 points · [117 comments](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242653)
- **Length**: 6.6K words (~29 min read)

These release notes are optimized for viewing inside Squeak, as they contain a lot of interactive examples. On this page, interactive links open in SqueakJS, which is a browser-based Smalltalk VM with some limitations. For the best experience, download Squeak and read the release notes there.

**\*\** Squeak 6.1 "Vanessa" Release Notes \*\*\***

As Squeak approaches its 30th anniversary, we are proud to announce the next version of the system, Squeak 6.1. Some highlights of this release are:

- A new [tree browser](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTreeBrowser%20fullOnClass%3A%20Object%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TreeBrowser fullOnClass: Object"), which enables navigation through classes and categories using refurbished hierarchical morphs,
- The return of [Objectland](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FObjectlandMorph%20openObjectland%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ObjectlandMorph openObjectland") (also known as the "Worlds of Squeak"),
- Several changes and fixes for the kernel infrastructure for simulating, unwinding, and scheduling processes and reshaping classes,
- Miscellaneous improvements and additions to the toolset and user interface for inspecting, debugging, profiling, and versioning code.

Please find below a detailed listing of all the changes, which includes notes on **Major Deprecations**, **Known Issues**, and **Compatibility Notes** at the bottom.

**About these release notes.** Phew, what a release! Since the last release *4 years ago*, we have merged *1700+ patches* with *9000+ method changes*. Beyond documenting technical changes in detail, these notes highlight the bigger picture and the overall impact of major developments. Along the way, you will find plenty of links to [code pointers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20class%3E%3E%23v61%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes class>>#v61") and [interactive examples](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%28Smalltalk%20isLowerPerformance%20or%3A%20%5BSqueakReleaseNotes%20doABarrelRoll%20isNil%5D%29%5Cr%20ifTrue%3A%20%5Bself%20inform%3A%20%27%27Hello%20world%21%27%27%5D.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotAssert.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Smalltalk isLowerPerformance or: [SqueakReleaseNotes doABarrelRoll isNil])  ifTrue: [self inform: 'Hello world!']. self flag: #doNotAssert."). Thus, these release notes serve not only as a changelog but also as a hands-on guide to exploring new features and mechanisms in Squeak. [Click here](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20viewOutline%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes viewOutline") to view an outline of this document.

In memory of Vanessa Freudenberg (1972-2025).

**/^(o.o)^\\ /^(o.o)^\\ /^(o.o)^\\**[](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20inform%3A%20%27%27Congratulations%2C%20you%20found%20an%20easter%20egg%21%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self inform: 'Congratulations, you found an easter egg!'") **/^(o.o)^\\ /^(o.o)^\\**

**Detailed Improvements in Language, Tools, and the Environment**

**~~~ GUI Frameworks ~~~**

*Morphic (up to* Morphic-ct.2218/MorphicTests-ct.97*)*

- **Major overhaul of** [**tree morphs**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28ObjectExplorer%20openOn%3A%20World%29%20extent%3A%20500px%40300px%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(ObjectExplorer openOn: World) extent: 500px@300px")**:**
  
  - Improves colors and [mouse and keyboard shortcuts](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28%28%28%28HelpBrowser%20openOn%3A%20SqueakTutorialsCommandKey%29%20model%20showFirstTopic%3B%20anyTextPaneWithSelector%3A%20%23topicContents%29%20update%3A%20%23inputRequested%20with%3A%20%23topicContents%3B%20textMorph%29%20editor%20setSearch%3A%20%27%27Trees%5C%5C%27%27%20withCRs%3B%20findAgainNow%3B%20morph%29%20editView%20scrollSelectionIntoView%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://((((HelpBrowser openOn: SqueakTutorialsCommandKey) model showFirstTopic; anyTextPaneWithSelector: #topicContents) update: #inputRequested with: #topicContents; textMorph) editor setSearch: 'Trees\' withCRs; findAgainNow; morph) editView scrollSelectionIntoView") for navigating trees.
  - Revises *type-to-filter* similar to lists, highlights search terms, and enables searching single columns via \[TAB]. Adds a [configurable filter mode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPluggableTreeMorph%3E%3E%23filterMode%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PluggableTreeMorph>>#filterMode:") to search within the current selection, all visible nodes, or the entire tree.
  - Introduces a recursive "find"/"find again" feature available via \[CMD] + \[F]/\[CMD] + \[G].
  - Adds a [flag](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSimpleHierarchicalListMorph%3E%3E%23autoExpand%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SimpleHierarchicalListMorph>>#autoExpand:") for automatic expansion of trees.
  - Improves support for *drag'n'drop* by automatically expanding nodes during dragging after one second of hovering.
  - Miscellaneous stability and performance improvements for layouting, rendering, and event handling in trees.
- In the world, supports dropping of [class references](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20grabTransferMorphFor%3A%20Fraction%20type%3A%20%23sourceCode%20from%3A%20self%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes grabTransferMorphFor: Fraction type: #sourceCode from: self"), "code://SqueakReleaseNotes grabTransferMorphFor: Smalltalk environment -> #'Kernel-Numbers' type: #sourceCode from: self") and [method categories](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20grabTransferMorphFor%3A%20Fraction%20-%3E%20%27%27printing%27%27%20type%3A%20%23sourceCode%20from%3A%20self%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes grabTransferMorphFor: Fraction -> 'printing' type: #sourceCode from: self"), and [inspector](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20grabTransferMorphFor%3A%20%28%28Inspector%20on%3A%20self%20currentHand%29%20fields%20detect%3A%20%5B%3Aea%20%7C%20ea%20name%20%3D%20%27%27bounds%27%27%5D%29%20type%3A%20%23inspectorField%20from%3A%20self%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes grabTransferMorphFor: ((Inspector on: self currentHand) fields detect: [:ea | ea name = 'bounds']) type: #inspectorField from: self") and [explorer fields](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%7C%20explorer%20%7C%5Crexplorer%20%3A%3D%20ObjectExplorer%20explore%3A%20Project%20current%20world.%5Crexplorer%20currentParent%3A%20explorer%20root%3B%20currentSelection%3A%20%28%28explorer%20rootObject%20explorerContents%20detect%3A%20%5B%3Aea%20%7C%20ea%20itemName%20%3D%20%27%27submorphs%27%27%5D%29%29%20item.%5CrSqueakReleaseNotes%20grabTransferMorphFor%3A%20explorer%20currentSelection%20type%3A%20%23explorerField%20from%3A%20%28%28ToolBuilder%20build%3A%20explorer%29%20findA%3A%20PluggableTreeMorph%29%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://| explorer | explorer := ObjectExplorer explore: Project current world. explorer currentParent: explorer root; currentSelection: ((explorer rootObject explorerContents detect: [:ea | ea itemName = 'submorphs'])) item. SqueakReleaseNotes grabTransferMorphFor: explorer currentSelection type: #explorerField from: ((ToolBuilder build: explorer) findA: PluggableTreeMorph)") to display them in a new window/widget.
- For mouse interactions, adds new preferences to customize the [double-click time](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20HandMorph%20getter%3A%20%23doubleClickTime%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: HandMorph getter: #doubleClickTime) open") and [drag thresholds](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20HandMorph%20getter%3A%20%23dragThreshold%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: HandMorph getter: #dragThreshold) open").
- In the [docking bar window menu](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Windows%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20nil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Windows'] filterItem: nil"), adds an item to collapse windows. Makes ["find workspace"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Windows%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27find%20workspace%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Windows'] filterItem: 'find workspace'") easier to use by expanding the selected window if collapsed. Reorders and decorates items in the [help menu](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Help%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20nil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Help'] filterItem: nil").
- Text editors:
  
  - Underlines [links (like this one!)](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Project%20uiManager%20chooseOptionFromLabeledValues%3A%20%28OrderedDictionary%20new%5Cr%20at%3A%20%27%27OMG%2C%20yes%21%27%27%20put%3A%20%5BHelpBrowser%20open%20model%20selectTopicLikePath%3A%20%7BSqueakHelp%20asHelpTopic.%20SqueakTutorials%20asHelpTopic.%20SqueakTutorials%20asHelpTopic%20subtopicAt%3A%20%23textLinks%7D%5D%3B%5Cr%20at%3A%20%27%27Uhm%2C%20let%20me%20reconsider%27%27%20put%3A%20nil%3B%5Cr%20yourself%29%20title%3A%20%27%27Yes%2C%20this%20is%20a%20link.%20You%20just%20clicked%20it%20successfully%21%5Cr%5CrDo%20you%20want%20to%20build%20your%20own%20link%20now%3F%27%27%29%20value%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Project uiManager chooseOptionFromLabeledValues: (OrderedDictionary new  at: 'OMG, yes!' put: [HelpBrowser open model selectTopicLikePath: {SqueakHelp asHelpTopic. SqueakTutorials asHelpTopic. SqueakTutorials asHelpTopic subtopicAt: #textLinks}];  at: 'Uhm, let me reconsider' put: nil;  yourself) title: 'Yes, this is a link. You just clicked it successfully!  Do you want to build your own link now?') value") during hovering and makes clicking and text selection through them more convenient.
  - When [enclosing/unenclosing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20TextEditor%20getter%3A%20%23encloseSelection%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: TextEditor getter: #encloseSelection) open") a selection in single/double quotes, nested quotes are now automatically escaped/unescaped.
- Morphic API: Allows morphs to [opt out of individual halo events](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMorph%3E%3E%23wantsHaloFromClickEvent%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Morph>>#wantsHaloFromClickEvent:") and to specify a balloon text via a [block](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllCallsOn%3A%20%23balloonTextBlock%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseAllCallsOn: #balloonTextBlock") instead of a selector.
- Menu construction:
  
  - Adds new utilities for adding [item groups](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMenuMorph%3E%3E%23addGroup%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MenuMorph>>#addGroup:") and [temporarily switching action targets](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMenuMorph%3E%3E%23withTarget%3Ado%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MenuMorph>>#withTarget:do:") during construction.
  - [#addList:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMenuMorph%3E%3E%23addList%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MenuMorph>>#addList:") now interprets backslashes in help tuples as line breaks.
  - Automatically trims trailing lines between menu items.
- Revises the debug menus of several morphs by adding a [time-profiler shortcut for buttons](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%7C%20window%20%7C%5CrSmalltalk%20isMorphic%20ifFalse%3A%20%5B%5E%20self%20inform%3A%20%27%27Can%20only%20demonstrate%20this%20in%20Morphic%27%27%5D.%5Crself%20inform%3A%20%27%27A%20system%20browser%20will%20open%20now.%3Cbr%3E%5CrFrom%20the%20%3Cb%3Ehalo%3C%2Fb%3E%20of%20one%20of%20the%20buttons%2C%20press%20the%20%3Cb%3Ewrench%20icon%3C%2Fb%3E%20on%20the%20right%20to%20open%20the%20%3Ci%3Edebug%20menu%3C%2Fi%3E%20and%20choose%20%3Cb%3E%5C%22spy%20on%20action%20invocation%5C%22%3C%2Fb%3E.%27%27%20asTextFromHtml.%5Crwindow%20%3A%3D%20%28Morph%3E%3E%23addAllMorphs%3A%29%20browse%20containingWindow.%5Cr%28window%20findDeepSubmorphThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20%28m%20isKindOf%3A%20PluggableButtonMorph%29%20and%3A%20%5Bm%20label%20%3D%20%27%27versions%27%27%20translated%5D%5D%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5Bnil%5D%29%20addHalo%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://| window | Smalltalk isMorphic ifFalse: [^ self inform: 'Can only demonstrate this in Morphic']. self inform: 'A system browser will open now.<br> From the <b>halo</b> of one of the buttons, press the <b>wrench icon</b> on the right to open the <i>debug menu</i> and choose <b>\"spy on action invocation\"</b>.' asTextFromHtml. window := (Morph>>#addAllMorphs:) browse containingWindow. (window findDeepSubmorphThat: [:m | (m isKindOf: PluggableButtonMorph) and: [m label = 'versions' translated]] ifAbsent: [nil]) addHalo"), providing access to the list and [tree contents](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%7C%20window%20%7C%5CrSmalltalk%20isMorphic%20ifFalse%3A%20%5B%5E%20self%20inform%3A%20%27%27Can%20only%20demonstrate%20this%20in%20Morphic%27%27%5D.%5Crwindow%20%3A%3D%20%28SystemWindow%20windowsIn%3A%20Project%20current%20world%20satisfying%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20model%20isKindOf%3A%20HelpBrowser%5D%29%5Cr%20at%3A%201%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5B%5E%20self%20inform%3A%20%27%27No%20open%20help%20browser%20found%27%27%5D.%5Crself%20inform%3A%20%27%27A%20halo%20on%20the%20tree%20morph%20in%20this%20window%20will%20open%20now.%5CrPress%20the%20%3Cb%3Ewrench%20icon%3C%2Fb%3E%20on%20the%20right%20to%20open%20the%20%3Ci%3Edebug%20menu%3C%2Fi%3E%20and%20choose%20%3Cb%3E%5C%22browse%20get-roots%20code%5C%22%3C%2Fb%3E%20or%20%3Cb%3E%5C%22debug%20get-roots%20invocation%5C%22%3C%2Fb%3E.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%28Tip%3A%20You%20can%20also%20open%20a%20halo%20on%20an%20individual%20tree%20node%20to%20explore%20its%20get-children%20logic%20in%20a%20similar%20way.%29%27%27%20asTextFromHtml.%5Cr%28window%20findDeepSubmorphThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20isKindOf%3A%20PluggableTreeMorph%5D%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5Bnil%5D%29%20addHalo%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://| window | Smalltalk isMorphic ifFalse: [^ self inform: 'Can only demonstrate this in Morphic']. window := (SystemWindow windowsIn: Project current world satisfying: [:m | m model isKindOf: HelpBrowser])  at: 1 ifAbsent: [^ self inform: 'No open help browser found']. self inform: 'A halo on the tree morph in this window will open now. Press the <b>wrench icon</b> on the right to open the <i>debug menu</i> and choose <b>\"browse get-roots code\"</b> or <b>\"debug get-roots invocation\"</b>.<br> <br> (Tip: You can also open a halo on an individual tree node to explore its get-children logic in a similar way.)' asTextFromHtml. (window findDeepSubmorphThat: [:m | m isKindOf: PluggableTreeMorph] ifAbsent: [nil]) addHalo"), and making existing "browse action" and "debug action" commands faster and more reliable.
- Honors the preference ["Open Tools Attached to Mouse Cursor"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20UIManager%20getter%3A%20%23openToolsAttachedToMouseCursor%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: UIManager getter: #openToolsAttachedToMouseCursor) open") in [Morph&gt;&gt;openAsTool](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMorph%3E%3E%23openAsTool%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Morph>>#openAsTool").
- Lots of UI tweaks and stability improvements:
  
  - Improves [theme](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27theme%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'theme'") support for [progress bars](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleProgressThemes.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleProgressThemes. self flag: #doNotTest."), text morph menus, and dialog windows.
  - Improves [high-DPI](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27scale%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'scale'") support for buttons, scroll panes, sliders, menus, multi-selection lists, trees, drop-shadows, the scratch pad, the keyboard exerciser, and others.
  - Improves [multilingual support](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllCallsOn%3A%20%23translated%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseAllCallsOn: #translated") in different places.
  - Fixes slips regarding the [world display depth menu](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTheWorldMenu%20new%20setDisplayDepth.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest%20%5C%22modal%5C%22%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D 'code://TheWorldMenu new setDisplayDepth. self flag: #doNotTest "modal"'), [clock](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%28World%20findDeeplyA%3A%20ClockMorph%29%5Cr%20ifNil%3A%20%5B%5EClockMorph%5D%5Cr%20ifNotNil%3A%20%5B%3Ac%20%7C%20c%20inspect%3B%20addHalo%5D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (World findDeeplyA: ClockMorph)  ifNil: [^ClockMorph]  ifNotNil: [:c | c inspect; addHalo]") configuration changes, [search-bar print-its](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleSearchBarPrintIt%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleSearchBarPrintIt"), [project view morph](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProjectViewMorph%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ProjectViewMorph") styling, and [transcript counting](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F3%20timesRepeat%3A%20%5BTranscript%20open%5D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://3 timesRepeat: [Transcript open]").
  - [Fixes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Morphic-mt.2217%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Morphic-mt.2217'") an issue when profiling code where user events were swallowed.
  - Several layouting fixes regarding [proportional layouts](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Morphic-mt.2018%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Morphic-mt.2018'"), [text morphs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27TextMorph%20minHeight%27%27%20%27%27TextMorph%20minWidth%27%27%20%27%27TextMorph%20doLayoutIn%3A%27%27%20%27%27TextMorph%20addCustomMenuItems%3Ahand%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27TextMorph%20layout%20fixes%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('TextMorph minHeight' 'TextMorph minWidth' 'TextMorph doLayoutIn:' 'TextMorph addCustomMenuItems:hand:') name: 'TextMorph layout fixes'"), [scroll panes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Morphic-mt.2129%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Morphic-mt.2129'"), and [transformation morphs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTransformationMorph%3E%3E%23extent%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TransformationMorph>>#extent:").
  - Fixes rendering issues with [transform morphs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20TransformMorph%3E%3E%23invalidRect%3Afrom%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: TransformMorph>>#invalidRect:from:"), [polygons](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20PolygonMorph%3E%3E%23computeBounds%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: PolygonMorph>>#computeBounds"), multi-selection lists, and trees.
  - Fixes scrolling in [nested scroll panes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FScrollPane%20exampleNested%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ScrollPane exampleNested").
  - In text morphs, improves stability of text selection and tweaks indentation regarding empty and blank lines.
  - Fixes ["browse senders"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextEditor%3E%3E%23sendersOfIt%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextEditor>>#sendersOfIt") of true, false, and nil, adds missing [access to workspace bindings](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPluggableTextMorph%3E%3E%23bindingOf%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PluggableTextMorph>>#bindingOf:") during accept-its, and improves robustness of [editor history](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextEditor%3E%3E%23closeTypeIn%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextEditor>>#closeTypeIn").
  - Fixes bugs in construction of [text containers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextContainer%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextContainer") and [conversion of paragraphs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FNewParagraph%3E%3E%23asText%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://NewParagraph>>#asText") to texts.
  - In lists, fixes automatic selection before dragging an item.
  - Fixes incorrect yellow-button menu invocation on buttons and lists.
  - Fixes a bug during menu construction, which depended on [uninitialized state](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMenuMorph%3E%3E%23addAllFrom%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MenuMorph>>#addAllFrom:").
  - Fixes scaling of [color forms](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FColorForm%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ColorForm").
  - Fixes [introspection of event handlers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMorph%3E%3E%23showActions%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Morph>>#showActions") in morph meta-menus.
  - Improves [compatibility](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27BalloonMorph%20text%27%27%20%27%27BalloonMorph%20setText%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27NewBalloonMorph%20compatibility%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('BalloonMorph text' 'BalloonMorph setText:') name: 'NewBalloonMorph compatibility'") between [old](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBalloonMorph%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://BalloonMorph") [and](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20NewBalloonMorph%20getter%3A%20%23useNewBalloonMorph%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: NewBalloonMorph getter: #useNewBalloonMorph) open") [new balloon morphs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FNewBalloonMorph%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://NewBalloonMorph").
  - Fixes a [remarkable slip](https://lists.squeakfoundation.org/archives/list/squeak-dev%40lists.squeakfoundation.org/thread/RNK3IM45WLMN5QVY3J3FFUEMPZYZNMS5/) that (1) prevented collapsed windows from applying a new UI theme and (2) introduced a possibly significant memory leak of windows.
  - Fixes [an issue on macOS](https://github.com/squeak-smalltalk/squeak-object-memory/issues/20) where calling the VM with additional command line arguments or plugging in external devices led to an incorrect "The VM is configured as a singleton application" warning.
  - Improves stability and backward compatibility when [loading](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBorderedMorph%3E%3E%23readDataFrom%3Asize%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://BorderedMorph>>#readDataFrom:size:") morphs from files.
  - Fixes cross-compatibility and concurrency issues with [MVC](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMVCProject%20new%20enter.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MVCProject new enter. self flag: #doNotTest").
  - Improves reliability of halos.
- Improves performance of large tree widgets, [transformation morphs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTransformationMorph%3E%3E%23extent%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TransformationMorph>>#extent:"), and [animated GIFs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAnimatedImageMorph%3E%3E%23step%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AnimatedImageMorph>>#step").
- Miscellaneous clean-ups and documentation improvements for [input events](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUserInputEvent%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UserInputEvent"), editor attributes, and others.

*MorphicExtras (up to* MorphicExtras-ct.360*)*

- **Introduces** [**Objectland**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Apps%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27Objectland%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Apps'] filterItem: 'Objectland'")**, which is a reconstruction of the famous "Worlds of Squeak" from 2003** and offers a colorful collection of examples that demonstrate the multifarious capabilities of Squeak. Re-adds the classic [Etoys race car example](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28HelpBrowser%20open%29%20model%20rootTopic%3A%20SqueakTutorials%20asHelpTopic%3B%20selectTopicLikePath%3A%20%7BSqueakTutorialsEToys%20asHelpTopic.%20SqueakTutorialsEToys%20makingAnEtoy%7D.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23workaround.%20%5C%22no%20%23openOn%3A%20because%20help%20browser%20commits%20to%20horizontal%20scroll%20bars%20initially%5C%22%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D 'code://(HelpBrowser open) model rootTopic: SqueakTutorials asHelpTopic; selectTopicLikePath: {SqueakTutorialsEToys asHelpTopic. SqueakTutorialsEToys makingAnEtoy}. self flag: #workaround. "no #openOn: because help browser commits to horizontal scroll bars initially"') and adds several new examples.
- After 18 years of spotless, clean Squeak, reintroduces and updates the famous [BlobMorph](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBlobMorph%20new%20openInHand%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://BlobMorph new openInHand").
- For [webcams](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWebCamMorph%20new%20openInHand%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WebCamMorph new openInHand"), adds a mirror option and extends interface for double-buffering. Improves UI and stability of webcam morph.
- Improves instrument menu of [score players](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FScorePlayerMorph%20extraExample%20openInHand%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ScorePlayerMorph extraExample openInHand").
- Fixes the ["preserve trash"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20TrashCanMorph%20getter%3A%20%23preserveTrash%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: TrashCanMorph getter: #preserveTrash) open") preference and improves the robustness of the [piano keyboard](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPianoKeyboardMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PianoKeyboardMorph new openInWorld").
- Improves high-DPI support for the [objects tool](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProject%20current%20world%20activateObjectsTool%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Project current world activateObjectsTool"), several of its tools such as [event recorders](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FEventRecorderMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://EventRecorderMorph new openInWorld"), [score players](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FScorePlayerMorph%20extraExample%20openInHand%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ScorePlayerMorph extraExample openInHand"), [recording controls](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRecordingControlsMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RecordingControlsMorph new openInWorld"), and [piano keyboards](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPianoKeyboardMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PianoKeyboardMorph new openInWorld"), and several EToys-related morphs.

*Etoys (up to* EToys-ct.532*)*

- Restores and revises a [tutorial on painting and creating scripts in Etoys](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FHelpBrowser%20openOn%3A%20SqueakTutorialsEToys%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://HelpBrowser openOn: SqueakTutorialsEToys").
- Adds an undo button to [FreeCell](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFreeCell%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FreeCell new openInWorld").
- Improves state indication in [Tetris](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTetris%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Tetris new openInWorld").
- Improves instrument menu of the [midi input morph](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMidiInputMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MidiInputMorph new openInWorld").
- Improves robustness of the [players gallery](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWorld%20galleryOfPlayers%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://World galleryOfPlayers"), the [spectrum analyzer](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSpectrumAnalyzerMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SpectrumAnalyzerMorph new openInWorld"), and the [midi input morph](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMidiInputMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MidiInputMorph new openInWorld"). Fixes changing the heading of a player.
- Clean-ups and fixes for cards and stacks.
- Improves high-DPI support for Etoys icons, tile morphs and script editors, [all games](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20browseGames%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes browseGames"), the [objects tool](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProject%20current%20world%20activateObjectsTool%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Project current world activateObjectsTool"), the [spectrum analyzer](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSpectrumAnalyzerMorph%20new%20openInWorld%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SpectrumAnalyzerMorph new openInWorld"), file lists, [watches](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWatchMorph%20new%20openInHand%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WatchMorph new openInHand"), and [calendars](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FCalendarMorph%20new%20openInHand%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://CalendarMorph new openInHand").
- Minor speedups for Kedama and players.
- Several clean-ups and refactorings that improve overall modularity and avoid unintended dependencies of other packages on Etoys.

*ST80 (up to* ST80-mt.311*)*

- Adds a close item to the [world menu](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleMVCProjectScreenMenu.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleMVCProjectScreenMenu. self flag: #doNotTest.").
- Improves high-DPI support for system views.
- Adds [View&gt;&gt;imageForm](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FView%3E%3E%23imageForm%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://View>>#imageForm") for taking a screenshot of a view and displays it in inspectors.
- Adds editor shortcut \[CMD] + \[SHIFT] + \[D] for "debug it" (already exists in Morphic).
- Miscellaneous fixes and stability improvements:
  
  - Ensures confirmation dialog before closing the current MVC project.
  - Prevents unexpected author-initials dialog when opening the ST80 world menu for the first time.
  - Avoids an issue with stale deferred actions in an empty MVC world, which caused emergency debuggers to appear only after pressing a mouse button.
  - In text editors, fixes pretty-printing and spawning if no compatible model is available. Gently handles nil selection provided by the model. Fixes an issue with recently introduced keyboard shortcuts.
  - Repairs [folder choosers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMVCProject%3E%3E%23findAFolderForProject%3Alabel%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MVCProject>>#findAFolderForProject:label:").

*CommandLine (up to* CommandLine-eem.27*)*

- Repairs the preference ["Save snapshot of image on failure"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20CommandLineToolSet%20getter%3A%20%23saveSnapshotOnError%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: CommandLineToolSet getter: #saveSnapshotOnError) open").
- Improves [UIManager support](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDummyUIManager%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DummyUIManager") for various file dialogs, font dialogs, confirmations, and requests.
- For interactive dialog requests, improves the error message before quitting.

**~~~ Programming & Tools ~~~**

*Tools (up to* Tools-mt.1366/ToolsTests-ct.137*)*

- **Introduces a new** [**tree browser**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTreeBrowser%20fullOnClass%3A%20Object%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TreeBrowser fullOnClass: Object"), which displays system categories, classes, and message categories in hierarchical panes and integrates Monticello packages. There are also [five new preferences](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPreferenceBrowser%20open%20searchPattern%3A%20%23TreeBrowser%3B%20changed%3A%20%23searchPatternNeverTriggered%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PreferenceBrowser open searchPattern: #TreeBrowser; changed: #searchPatternNeverTriggered") for configuring its appearance.
  
  - **Reworks *drag'n'drop*** for tree browsers and traditional browsers to support [all meaningful combinations of panes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%27%27Possible%20drag%27%27%27%27n%27%27%27%27drop%20operations%20you%20can%20try%20out%20in%20system%20browsers%3A%5Cr*%20Drag%20methods%20or%20method%20categories%20onto%20a%20class%20to%20move%20or%20copy%20them%20there%20%28previously%2C%20you%20could%20only%20drop%20a%20method%20onto%20a%20message%20category%20in%20a%20second%20browser%29.%5Cr*%20In%20tree%20browsers%2C%20drag%20a%20category%20onto%20another%20category%20to%20insert%20it%20as%20a%20child%20category.%5Cr*%20In%20tree%20browsers%2C%20when%20renaming%20or%20moving%20a%20category%2C%20have%20all%20its%20children%20automatically%20renamed%2Fmoved%20as%20well%20%28holds%20up%20the%20tree%20browser%27%27%27%27s%20files-and-folders%20metaphor%29.%5Cr*%20Also%20in%20tree%20browsers%2C%20shift-drag%20a%20class%20onto%20a%20system%20category%20to%20copy%20it.%5Cr*%20Drag%20a%20class%20onto%20another%20class%20to%20change%20its%20superclass.%5Cr*%20Drag%20a%20category%20onto%20--%20all%20--%20to%20dissolve%20it%20%28i.e.%2C%20unclassify%20all%20its%20elements%29.%5Cr*%20Move%20or%20copy%20classes%20and%20system%20categories%20between%20different%20environments.%5Cr*%20When%20renaming%20a%20system%20category%2C%20a%20dialog%20will%20offer%20you%20to%20recategorize%20any%20extension%20methods%20as%20well.%5Cr*%20Drag%20method%20categories%20or%20system%20categories%20into%20the%20world%20to%20spawn%20a%20new%20browser%20on%20them.%5Cr*%20Drag%20and%20drop%20from%2Finto%20singleton%20browsers%20%28that%20show%20a%20single%20class%20or%20category%29%20just%20like%20in%20full%20browsers.%27%27%5Credit%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// 'Possible drag''n''drop operations you can try out in system browsers: * Drag methods or method categories onto a class to move or copy them there (previously, you could only drop a method onto a message category in a second browser). * In tree browsers, drag a category onto another category to insert it as a child category. * In tree browsers, when renaming or moving a category, have all its children automatically renamed/moved as well (holds up the tree browser''s files-and-folders metaphor). * Also in tree browsers, shift-drag a class onto a system category to copy it. * Drag a class onto another class to change its superclass. * Drag a category onto -- all -- to dissolve it (i.e., unclassify all its elements). * Move or copy classes and system categories between different environments. * When renaming a system category, a dialog will offer you to recategorize any extension methods as well. * Drag method categories or system categories into the world to spawn a new browser on them. * Drag and drop from/into singleton browsers (that show a single class or category) just like in full browsers.' edit") for copying or moving classes, methods, and categories.
  - Adds double-click support for [hierarchy tree browsers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTreeBrowser%20fullHierarchyOnClass%3A%20Collection%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TreeBrowser fullHierarchyOnClass: Collection") to switch the central class. This is especially fun for [multi-inheritance hierarchies with traits](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleTreeBrowserMultipleInheritanceWithTraits%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleTreeBrowserMultipleInheritanceWithTraits").
  - In the class pane menu, adds [new items](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%7C%20browser%20classList%20menu%20%7C%5Crbrowser%20%3A%3D%20TreeBrowser%20fullOnClass%3A%20Object.%5CrclassList%20%3A%3D%20browser%20containingWindow%20findDeepSubmorphThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20knownName%20%3D%20%23classList%5D%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5B%5E%20nil%5D.%5CrclassList%20yellowButtonActivity%3A%20false.%5Crmenu%20%3A%3D%20self%20currentWorld%20findA%3A%20MenuMorph.%5Crmenu%20position%3A%20classList%20position%20%2B%2015%20px.%5Crmenu%20checkedMatchString%3A%20%27%27new%27%27.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// | browser classList menu | browser := TreeBrowser fullOnClass: Object. classList := browser containingWindow findDeepSubmorphThat: [:m | m knownName = #classList] ifAbsent: [^ nil]. classList yellowButtonActivity: false. menu := self currentWorld findA: MenuMorph. menu position: classList position + 15 px. menu checkedMatchString: 'new'.") for generating a new class or trait.
  - In the message category menu, adds [items](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%7C%20browser%20messageCatList%20menu%20%7C%5Crbrowser%20%3A%3D%20TreeBrowser%20fullOnClass%3A%20Object%20category%3A%20%27%27accessing%27%27.%5CrmessageCatList%20%3A%3D%20browser%20containingWindow%20findDeepSubmorphThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20knownName%20%3D%20%23messageCategoryList%5D%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5B%5E%20nil%5D.%5CrmessageCatList%20yellowButtonActivity%3A%20false.%5Crmenu%20%3A%3D%20self%20currentWorld%20findA%3A%20MenuMorph.%5Crmenu%20position%3A%20messageCatList%20position%20%2B%2015%20px.%5Crmenu%20checkedMatchString%3A%20%27%27browse%27%27.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// | browser messageCatList menu | browser := TreeBrowser fullOnClass: Object category: 'accessing'. messageCatList := browser containingWindow findDeepSubmorphThat: [:m | m knownName = #messageCategoryList] ifAbsent: [^ nil]. messageCatList yellowButtonActivity: false. menu := self currentWorld findA: MenuMorph. menu position: messageCatList position + 15 px. menu checkedMatchString: 'browse'.") to browse all \[related] message categories in the system.
  - In the annotation pane of class definitions, adds a [shortcut](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28%28TreeBrowser%20fullOnClass%3A%20Object%29%20containingWindow%20findDeepSubmorphThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20isTextView%20and%3A%20%5Bm%20getTextSelector%20%3D%20%23annotation%5D%5D%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5B%5E%20nil%5D%29%20addHalo%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://((TreeBrowser fullOnClass: Object) containingWindow findDeepSubmorphThat: [:m | m isTextView and: [m getTextSelector = #annotation]] ifAbsent: [^ nil]) addHalo") for reading the full class comment.
  - Simplifies [extension point](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FStandardToolSet%20class%3E%3E%23browseHierarchy%3Aselector%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://StandardToolSet class>>#browseHierarchy:selector:") for custom browsers to register as hierarchy browsers.
  - Improves [multi-environment support](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FObject%20newSubclass%5Cr%20in%3A%20%5B%3Ac%20%7C%20c%20environment%20import%3A%20Smalltalk%20environment%5D%3B%5Cr%20comment%3A%20%27%27I%20am%20a%20uniclass%20in%20a%20custom%20environment%21%27%27%3B%5Cr%20browse%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Object newSubclass  in: [:c | c environment import: Smalltalk environment];  comment: 'I am a uniclass in a custom environment!';  browse") for all kinds of browsers and message sets.
- In the Message Names tool, shows the definition, comment, and hierarchy for [class results](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMessageNames%3A%20%27%27SequenceableCollection%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMessageNames: 'SequenceableCollection'").
- **Extends inspector support for custom fields:**
  
  - Allows objects to contribute **custom** [**inspector fields**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28SystemBrowser%20default%20fullOnClass%3A%20Object%20selector%3A%20%23streamInspectorFieldsFor%3Aon%3A%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27inspect%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (SystemBrowser default fullOnClass: Object selector: #streamInspectorFieldsFor:on:) filterMessages: 'inspect'") **and** [**menu items**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28SystemBrowser%20default%20fullOnClass%3A%20Object%20selector%3A%20%23addInspectorItemsToMenu%3Afor%3A%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27inspect%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (SystemBrowser default fullOnClass: Object selector: #addInspectorItemsToMenu:for:) filterMessages: 'inspect'").
  - Adds new [meta (shift) menu](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%7C%20inspector%20fieldList%20menu%20%7C%5Crinspector%20%3A%3D%20Project%20current%20world%20inspectorClass%20openOn%3A%20Project%20current%20world.%5Crinspector%20model%20selectFieldNamed%3A%20%27%27extent%27%27.%5CrfieldList%20%3A%3D%20inspector%20findDeepSubmorphThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20knownName%20%3D%20%23fieldList%5D%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5B%5E%20nil%5D.%5CrfieldList%20yellowButtonActivity%3A%20true.%5Crmenu%20%3A%3D%20self%20currentWorld%20findA%3A%20MenuMorph.%5Crmenu%20position%3A%20fieldList%20position%20%2B%20%2815%20px%20%40%2030%20px%29.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// | inspector fieldList menu | inspector := Project current world inspectorClass openOn: Project current world. inspector model selectFieldNamed: 'extent'. fieldList := inspector findDeepSubmorphThat: [:m | m knownName = #fieldList] ifAbsent: [^ nil]. fieldList yellowButtonActivity: true. menu := self currentWorld findA: MenuMorph. menu position: fieldList position + (15 px @ 30 px).") to inspect fields or browse/debug their accessors.
  - Adds [InspectorField&gt;&gt;#printBlock:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%23printBlock%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://#printBlock:") for customizing the representation of a field independently of its underlying (inspectable) value.
- When [inspecting large collections](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%7C%20object%20%7C%5Crobject%20%3A%3D%20Text%20melvilleSample%20asString%20asArray.%5Cr%28ToolSet%20inspect%3A%20object%29%20model%20selectFieldNamed%3A%20%27%27...%27%27.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://| object | object := Text melvilleSample asString asArray. (ToolSet inspect: object) model selectFieldNamed: '...'."), allows disabling truncation to show all elements.
- Makes [inspection](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Tools-ct.1313%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Tools-ct.1313'") and [debugging](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Tools-ct.1314%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Tools-ct.1314'") of transparent proxy objects and unhashable objects more reliable by avoiding sending them any messages, to which they might react slowly or catastrophically.
- **Debuggers:**
  
  - **Adds** [**"send until…" command**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleDebuggerSendUntil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleDebuggerSendUntil") to precisely search for a condition in nested message sends.
  - **Improves** [**"run to here" command**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleDebuggerRunToHereBlock.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleDebuggerRunToHereBlock. self flag: #doNotTest.") to reach expressions in blocks.
  - Improves error handling for invalid run until/send until expressions. For ["return entered value"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleDebuggerReturnEnteredValue%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleDebuggerReturnEnteredValue"), uses the proper evaluatorClass of the receiver.
  - To the stack list menu, adds ["select home" option](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleDebuggerSelectHome.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleDebuggerSelectHome. self flag: #doNotTest.") for finding the home contexts of (possibly deeply nested) blocks.
  - **Makes the signaled exception and suspended process accessible** in the debugger when no context frame is selected. [Try it out!](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%7Cworkspace%7C%5Crworkspace%20%3A%3D%20Project%20uiManager%20edit%3A%20%27%271.%20Run%20the%20following%20do-it%20via%20%3Cb%3ECmd%20%2B%20P%3C%2Fb%3E%3Cbr%3E%5Cr2.%20In%20the%20debugger%20notifier%2C%20click%20on%20%3Cb%3EDebug%3C%2Fb%3E%20to%20open%20a%20full%20debugger%3Cbr%3E%5Cr3.%20Click%20on%20the%20%3Cb%3Ecurrently%20selected%20context%20frame%3C%2Fb%3E%20again%20to%20%3Cb%3Edeselect%3C%2Fb%3E%20it%3Cbr%3E%5Cr4.%20In%20the%20text%20pane%2C%20now%20the%20%3Cb%3E%3Cfont%20color%3D%5C%22%23008000%5C%22%3Eoriginal%20error%20message%3C%2Ffont%3E%3C%2Fb%3E%20is%20shown%21%3Cbr%3E%5Cr5.%20By%20using%20the%20two%20%3Cb%3Einspectors%3C%2Fb%3E%20at%20the%20bottom%2C%20you%20can%20also%20inspect%20the%20exception%20and%20the%20suspended%20process.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr6.%20Alternatively%2C%20you%20can%20also%20toggle%20between%20the%20exception%2Fprocess%20view%20and%20normal%20stacks%20via%20the%20%3Cb%3Esmall%20window%20menu%20icon%3C%2Fb%3E%20at%20the%20right%20top%20corner%20of%20the%20window.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%20%7C%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%20%7C%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%20v%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%26nbsp%3B%3Cfont%20color%3D%5C%22%23800000%5C%22%3E2%3C%2Ffont%3E%3Cfont%20color%3D%5C%22%23000000%5C%22%3E%20%3C%2Ffont%3E%3Cfont%20color%3D%5C%22%23000080%5C%22%3E%2F%3C%2Ffont%3E%3Cfont%20color%3D%5C%22%23000000%5C%22%3E%20%3C%2Ffont%3E%3Cfont%20color%3D%5C%22%23800000%5C%22%3E0%3C%2Ffont%3E%27%27%20asTextFromHtml%20label%3A%20%27%27Tutorial%3A%20Viewing%20exception%20and%20process%20in%20debugger%27%27%20shouldStyle%3A%20false.%5CrToolBuilder%20default%20class%20setBoundsForWindow%3A%20workspace%20to%3A%20%28%28%28ToolBuilder%20default%20class%2
