0getBoundsForWindow%3A%20workspace%29%20origin%20extent%3A%20475px%40300px%29%29.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://|workspace| workspace := Project uiManager edit: '1. Run the following do-it via <b>Cmd + P</b><br> 2. In the debugger notifier, click on <b>Debug</b> to open a full debugger<br> 3. Click on the <b>currently selected context frame</b> again to <b>deselect</b> it<br> 4. In the text pane, now the <b><font color=\"#008000\">original error message</font></b> is shown!<br> 5. By using the two <b>inspectors</b> at the bottom, you can also inspect the exception and the suspended process.<br> 6. Alternatively, you can also toggle between the exception/process view and normal stacks via the <b>small window menu icon</b> at the right top corner of the window.<br> <br> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; |<br> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; |<br> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; v<br> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<font color=\"#800000\">2</font><font color=\"#000000\"> </font><font color=\"#000080\">/</font><font color=\"#000000\"> </font><font color=\"#800000\">0</font>' asTextFromHtml label: 'Tutorial: Viewing exception and process in debugger' shouldStyle: false. ToolBuilder default class setBoundsForWindow: workspace to: (((ToolBuilder default class getBoundsForWindow: workspace) origin extent: 475px@300px)).")
  - **Adds a** [**byteCodes mode**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleDebuggerByteCodes%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleDebuggerByteCodes") **to the debugger** to view and step through the single VM instructions of a method (similar to the byteCodes mode in browsers). Adds a new "simulate" button to debug how Squeak's metacircular evaluator (aka context simulator) interprets the next bytecode. The new mode is reachable through the window menu &gt; "what to show…", or, when [extra debugger buttons](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20preferenceAt%3A%20%23extraDebuggerButtons%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences preferenceAt: #extraDebuggerButtons) open") are enabled, through the "source" button above the code pane.
  - Miscellaneous clean-ups and improvements for customizing [inspector classes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FContext%3E%3E%23contextVariablesInspectorClass%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Context>>#contextVariablesInspectorClass").
- **Welcomes [AndreasSystemProfiler](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27AndreasSystemProfiler%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'AndreasSystemProfiler Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new") in the trunk**, which was previously hosted in the VMMaker repository. Improves report format, robustness for tallying primitives, and documentation.
- In workspaces, adds a ["load contents from file…" option](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%7Cws%20menu%7C%5Crws%20%3A%3D%20Project%20uiManager%20edit%3A%20%27%27In%20workspaces%2C%20adds%20a%20%5C%22load%20contents%20from%20file...%5C%22%20option%20to%20the%20window%20menu.%27%27%20label%3A%20%27%27Workspace%27%27%20shouldStyle%3A%20false.%5Crws%20offerWindowMenu.%5Crmenu%20%3A%3D%20self%20currentWorld%20findA%3A%20MenuMorph.%5Crws%20topRight%3A%20menu%20topLeft%20%2B%20%2850%20px%20%40%20-50%20px%29.%5Crmenu%20checkedMatchString%3A%20%27%27load%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://|ws menu| ws := Project uiManager edit: 'In workspaces, adds a \"load contents from file...\" option to the window menu.' label: 'Workspace' shouldStyle: false. ws offerWindowMenu. menu := self currentWorld findA: MenuMorph. ws topRight: menu topLeft + (50 px @ -50 px). menu checkedMatchString: 'load'") to the window menu.
- **Improvements to** [**change recovery**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27Recover%20changes%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'Recover changes'")**:** Improves parsing of class definitions, method removals, recategorizations, and more. Adds and revises menu items to select all changes of a given type or filter for changes to the currently selected type or selector.
- Adds utilities [Process&gt;&gt;#browse](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcess%3E%3E%23browse%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Process>>#browse") and [Context&gt;&gt;#tempNamed:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FContext%3E%3E%23tempNamed%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Context>>#tempNamed:")\[[put:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FContext%3E%3E%23tempNamed%3Aput%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Context>>#tempNamed:put:")].
- Adds [file services](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FArchiveViewer%20class%3E%3E%23fileReaderServicesForFile%3Asuffix%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ArchiveViewer class>>#fileReaderServicesForFile:suffix:") for viewing compressed `csz` and `stz` archives.
- In [truncated file lists](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleBrowseSourcesFileText%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleBrowseSourcesFileText"), inserts a link for reading the entire file.
- Improves layout of [code-point browsers for single-byte text-converters](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FByteTextConverter%20class%3E%3E%23browseAllCodePoints%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ByteTextConverter class>>#browseAllCodePoints").
- Miscellaneous UI improvements and bug fixes:
  
  - Fixes for browsers:
    
    - Fixes shortcut inconsistency in single-category browsers.
    - Improves robustness in browsers when defining traits.
    - Fixes a slip in hierarchy browsers that spawned a new redundant browser when redefining the selected class.
    - Fixes [package pane browser](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPackagePaneBrowser%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PackagePaneBrowser open") to actually use the package organizer instead of parsing the system category list. If no category is selected, shows all classes from the package.
    - Deduplicates menus in package pane browsers.
    - Fixes missing UI updates and class-side do-its in package pane browsers and [dependency browsers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDependencyBrowser%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DependencyBrowser open").
    - Fixes UI proportions and styling of class definitions and comments in [file contents browsers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleFileContentsBrowser%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleFileContentsBrowser").
    - Consistently sorts classes in [class list browsers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FClassListBrowser%20example3.%20nil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ClassListBrowser example3. nil").
  - Fixes for message traces:
    
    - Ensures consistent method labels in message traces.
    - Improves robustness and dialogs for browsing senders/implementors.
    - Minor UI string fixes.
  - Fixes for message names:
    
    - Fixes spawn (\[CMD] + \[O]) in [message names](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMessageNames%20openMessageNames%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MessageNames openMessageNames").
  - Improvements for [versions browsers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20VersionsBrowser%3E%3E%23annotation%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: VersionsBrowser>>#annotation"):
    
    - Displays the stamp and category of the selected version, rather than always the current version's.
    - When filing out a method, adds missing file extension.
  - Fixes for [change lists](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27Recover%20changes%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'Recover changes'"):
    
    - Fixes change logging for methods that are recompiled from a browser without its existing category being selected.
    - Improves robustness of recover changes scanning.
    - Improves stability for "remove older method versions".
  - Fixes for inspectors and explorers:
    
    - Fixes inspecting of [forms](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28ToolSet%20inspect%3A%20Form%20extraWizard%29%20model%20selectFieldNamed%3A%20%27%27pixels%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(ToolSet inspect: Form extraWizard) model selectFieldNamed: 'pixels'") in non-native display depths.
    - Fixes nested temporary variables when [inspecting blocks](https://lists.squeakfoundation.org/archives/list/squeak-dev@lists.squeakfoundation.org/thread/B2YYJTTCEGOHZXIHOIKEXPTKNC4OM3JR).
    - Fixes inspecting dictionaries with [non-storable keys](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Tools-ct.1365%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Tools-ct.1365'").
    - Adds styling of *thisContext* in context inspectors in debuggers.
    - Fixes shortcut for opening an Etoys viewer (\[CMD] + \[V]).
    - [Improves robustness](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Tools-ct.1312%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Tools-ct.1312'") when exchanging the inspected object in an inspector.
    - Improves robustness of ["copy expression"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FInspectorField%20class%3E%3E%23generateExpressionFrom%3AargumentNames%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://InspectorField class>>#generateExpressionFrom:argumentNames:") for a selected field.
    - Fixes handling of unsaved changes in inspectors and explorers by using new [#okToChange:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Inspector%20okToChange%3A%27%27%20%27%27ObjectExplorer%20okToChange%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27okToChange%3A%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('Inspector okToChange:' 'ObjectExplorer okToChange:') name: 'okToChange:'") hook.
    - Harmonizes object explorer labels to match labels in the tree.
  - Fixes for debugger:
    
    - [Fixes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%23stepToStatement%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://#stepToStatement") stepping through abort messages such as [#mustBeBoolean](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23mustBeBoolean%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #mustBeBoolean") and restarting from primitive methods that no longer fail, and updates window label accordingly. Improves [tests](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%7BSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20DebuggerTests%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27abort%27%27.%5CrSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20DebuggerTests%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27unexpected%27%27%7D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://{SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (ToolSet browseClass: DebuggerTests) filterMessages: 'abort'. SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (ToolSet browseClass: DebuggerTests) filterMessages: 'unexpected'}").
    - Fixes highlighting of next expression for to:do: statements and assignments.
    - Fixes out-of-date context variables after restarting the top context.
    - Fixes regression in runUntil when pressing shift.
    - Fixes "return entered value" to properly detect unhandled errors during unwinding.
    - When recompiling a method in a debugger, allows to proceed despite of no active home method. Fixes bug when recompilation is aborted.
    - Improves stability and up-to-dateness of stack list.
    - Miscellaneous clean-ups.
- Speeds up [process browser](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcessBrowser%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ProcessBrowser open") for managing [very large lists](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcessBrowser%3E%3E%23updateProcessList%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ProcessBrowser>>#updateProcessList") of processes.
- Improves high-DPI support for system browsers and [FileList2](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27FileList2%20Hierarchy%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'FileList2 Hierarchy'; actOnClickFor: Model new").
- Miscellaneous improvements of multilingual support.
- Significantly improves test coverage of [tree browsers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTreeBrowserTest%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TreeBrowserTest") and [debuggers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDebuggerTests%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DebuggerTests").

*ToolBuilder (up to* ToolBuilder-Kernel-ct.176*/*ToolBuilder-Morphic-ct.370*/*ToolBuilder-MVC-mt.77*/*ToolBuilder-Tests-ct.9*)*

- Adds support for blocks in [ToolBuilderSpec&gt;&gt;help:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolBuilderSpec%3E%3E%23help%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolBuilderSpec>>#help:") and [PluggableMenuItemSpec&gt;&gt;action:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPluggableMenuItemSpec%3E%3E%23action%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PluggableMenuItemSpec>>#action:").
- Improves [compatibility](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20browseSharedProtocolOfAll%3A%20%7BMenuMorph.%20CustomMenu.%20PluggableMenuSpec%7D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes browseSharedProtocolOfAll: {MenuMorph. CustomMenu. PluggableMenuSpec}") between MenuMorphs, CustomMenus, and PluggableMenuSpec.
- Adds [UIManager&gt;&gt;inform:title:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUIManager%3E%3E%23inform%3Atitle%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UIManager>>#inform:title:") for also providing a title.
- Adds [UIManager&gt;&gt;chooseFileFrom:\[…\]](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28ToolSet%20browse%3A%20UIManager%20selector%3A%20%23chooseFileFrom%3A%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27chooseFileFrom%3A%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (ToolSet browse: UIManager selector: #chooseFileFrom:) filterMessages: 'chooseFileFrom:'") for providing a start directory.
- Adds shorthands [UIManager&gt;&gt;chooseFile](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUIManager%3E%3E%23chooseFile%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UIManager>>#chooseFile") and [UIManager&gt;&gt;multiLineRequest:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUIManager%3E%3E%23multiLineRequest%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UIManager>>#multiLineRequest:")\[[initialAnswer:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUIManager%3E%3E%23multiLineRequest%3AinitialAnswer%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UIManager>>#multiLineRequest:initialAnswer:")].
- For [UIManager&gt;&gt;informUser(](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28ToolSet%20browse%3A%20UIManager%20selector%3A%20%23informUserDuring%3A%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27informUser%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (ToolSet browse: UIManager selector: #informUserDuring:) filterMessages: 'informUser'")[:d](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUIManager%3E%3E%23informUser%3Aduring%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UIManager>>#informUser:during:")[|](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28ToolSet%20browse%3A%20UIManager%20selector%3A%20%23informUserDuring%3A%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27informUser%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (ToolSet browse: UIManager selector: #informUserDuring:) filterMessages: 'informUser'")[D](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUIManager%3E%3E%23informUserDuring%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UIManager>>#informUserDuring:")[)uring:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28ToolSet%20browse%3A%20UIManager%20selector%3A%20%23informUserDuring%3A%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27informUser%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (ToolSet browse: UIManager selector: #informUserDuring:) filterMessages: 'informUser'"), provides optional access to the progress bar inside the provided block and properly returns the result of the block.
- Stops ignoring help texts for checkboxes and menu items.
- Morphic-specific changes:
  
  - [**Improves**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27ToolBuilder-Morphic-ct.320%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'ToolBuilder-Morphic-ct.320'") **convenience and stability of** [**file**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Project%20uiManager%20chooseFileMatchingSuffixes%3A%20%23%28%27%27st%27%27%20%27%27cs%27%27%29%20label%3A%20%27%27Example%20file%20chooser%27%27%29%20ifNotNil%3A%20%23inspect%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Project uiManager chooseFileMatchingSuffixes: #('st' 'cs') label: 'Example file chooser') ifNotNil: #inspect") [**selection**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%28Project%20uiManager%20saveFilenameRequest%3A%20%27%27Example%20save%20file%20dialog%27%27%20initialAnswer%3A%20%27%27newName.st%27%27%29%20ifNotNil%3A%20%23inspect%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Project uiManager saveFilenameRequest: 'Example save file dialog' initialAnswer: 'newName.st') ifNotNil: #inspect") [**dialogs**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%28Project%20uiManager%20chooseDirectory%3A%20%27%27Example%20directory%20chooser%27%27%20from%3A%20%28FileDirectory%20on%3A%20FileDirectory%20workingDirectory%29%29%20ifNotNil%3A%20%23inspect%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Project uiManager chooseDirectory: 'Example directory chooser' from: (FileDirectory on: FileDirectory workingDirectory)) ifNotNil: #inspect")**.** All kinds of file dialogs now contain an address bar. Improves handling of extensions, automatic selection by typing into address bar, and handles double-click on a file to confirm the dialog.
  - In [lists](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%23wantsDroppedMorph%3Aevent%3Aon%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://#wantsDroppedMorph:event:on:") and [trees](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPluggableTreeMorph%3E%3E%23wantsDroppedMorph%3Aevent%3AonItem%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PluggableTreeMorph>>#wantsDroppedMorph:event:onItem:"), allows the model to decide whether it accepts a dropped object *based on the hovered item*.
  - Adds new model-changed operations for trees to [expand](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20PluggableTreeMorph%3E%3E%23update%3Awith%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: PluggableTreeMorph>>#update:with:") or [update the labels](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20PluggableTreeMorph%3E%3E%23update%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: PluggableTreeMorph>>#update:") of nodes. Forgives models that do not emit the current path for the current selection but fetches the new path automatically.
  - For tree specs, adds a new [filter mode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPluggableTreeSpec%3E%3E%23filterMode%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PluggableTreeSpec>>#filterMode:") and [search mode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPluggableTreeSpec%3E%3E%23searchMode%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PluggableTreeSpec>>#searchMode:").
  - Fixes [UI glitches](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27ToolBuilder-Morphic-ct.318%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'ToolBuilder-Morphic-ct.318'") when dynamically updating the children of windows and panels.
  - Clean-ups and deduplications for trees.
- MVC-specific changes:
  
  - Adds static support for [children selector](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPluggableCompositeSpec%3E%3E%23children%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PluggableCompositeSpec>>#children:") in panels analogously to windows (dynamic children updates in MVC are still not supported). Fixes a layout slip for zero children.
  - In [MVCToolBuilder&gt;&gt;open:label:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMVCToolBuilder%3E%3E%23open%3Alabel%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MVCToolBuilder>>#open:label:"), adds missing support for view and menu arguments.
  - Fixes UI glitches when closing windows with scroll bars.

*SUnit (up to* SUnit-mt.153/SUnitGUI-mt.95/SUnitTools-mt.9/ToolBuilder-SUnit-pre.22*)*

- **Indicates** [**timeouts**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTestFailure%3E%3E%23isTimeout%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TestFailure>>#isTimeout") in TestResult and displays them in the [test runner](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleTestRunnerTimeout%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleTestRunnerTimeout").
- Repairs the exclusion of classes via [#classNamesNotUnderTest](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27TestRunner%20excludeClassesNotUnderTestFrom%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27%23classNamesNotUnderTest%27%27%20autoSelect%3A%20%27%27classNamesNotUnderTest%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('TestRunner excludeClassesNotUnderTestFrom:') name: '#classNamesNotUnderTest' autoSelect: 'classNamesNotUnderTest'").
- Fixes test generation for #// selectors.
- Properly resets [unexpected passes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTestResult%3E%3E%23resetUnexpectedPasses%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TestResult>>#resetUnexpectedPasses") when rerunning failures in test runner.

*Nebraska (up to* Nebraska-ct.65*)*

- Adds support for [mouse-wheel scrolling](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FNetworkTerminalMorph%3E%3E%23handleMouseWheel%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://NetworkTerminalMorph>>#handleMouseWheel:") in remote terminals.
- Improves reliability of event handling for [remote terminals](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27NebraskaServer%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'NebraskaServer Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new"), making it possible to freely move, resize, and rotate terminals in the client's world again.
- Fixes default port selection for Nebraska connections.
- Improves error handling for refused peer-to-peer connections.

*Protocols (up to* Protocols-ct.91*)*

- Improves high-DPI support for [lexicons](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FLexicon%20browseFullProtocol.%20nil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Lexicon browseFullProtocol. nil").
- Fixes and cleans up window titles of lexicons.

*Services*

- No changes.

*Shout (Syntax Highlighting) (up to* ShoutCore-ct.98/ShoutTests-eem.34*)*

- **Styles comments in **italics** by default** and provides a [preference](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20SHTextStylerST80%20getter%3A%20%23enforceItalicEmphasisInComments%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: SHTextStylerST80 getter: #enforceItalicEmphasisInComments) open") for this behavior.
- Fixes parsing of unary selectors beginning and/or ending with [underscores](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20Scanner%20getter%3A%20%23allowUnderscoreAsAssignment%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: Scanner getter: #allowUnderscoreAsAssignment) open").

**~~~ Core Language ~~~**

*Kernel (up to* Kernel-ct.1688/KernelTests-ct.482*)*

- **Numbers:**
  
  - Adds new facilities [Complex&gt;&gt;log](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Complex%3E%3E%23log%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Complex>>#log"), [Integer&gt;&gt;bitInvert64](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Integer%3E%3E%23bitInvert64%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Integer>>#bitInvert64"), [Integer&gt;&gt;timesCollect:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Integer%3E%3E%23timesCollect%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Integer>>#timesCollect:"), [Number&gt;&gt;timesTwoPower:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Number%3E%3E%23timesTwoPower%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Number>>#timesTwoPower:"), and [ScaledDecimal&gt;&gt;printFractionAsDecimal](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ScaledDecimal%3E%3E%23printFractionAsDecimal%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ScaledDecimal>>#printFractionAsDecimal").
  - Corrects behavior for [#sin](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23sin%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #sin"), [#cos](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23cos%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #cos"), and [#tan](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23tan%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #tan") for huge integers and fractions.
  - Several fixes for number parsing ([rounding](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqNumberParserTest%3E%3E%23testIntegerWithNegativeExponent%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqNumberParserTest>>#testIntegerWithNegativeExponent") of floats with large negative exponent and no fraction part; parsing of [FORTRAN](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27FORTRANNumberParser%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'FORTRANNumberParser Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new") numbers).
  - Various optimizations for [integer bit manipulation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20Integer%20category%3A%20%27%27bit%20manipulation%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseClass: Integer category: 'bit manipulation'"), [mixed int-float arithmetic (+ - \*)](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloDiffFrom%3A%20%27%27Kernel-ct.1549%27%27%20to%3A%20%27%27Kernel-mt.1551%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloDiffFrom: 'Kernel-ct.1549' to: 'Kernel-mt.1551'"), [float-to-fraction conversion](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23asTrueFraction%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #asTrueFraction"), and [trigonometric functions](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23cos%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #cos").
- Adds support for **non-local exits** with values through [BlockClosure&gt;&gt;valueWithExitValue](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20BlockClosure%3E%3E%23valueWithExitValue%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// BlockClosure>>#valueWithExitValue").
- **Models:** Allows [unsaved changes checks](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FObject%3E%3E%23okToChange%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Object>>#okToChange:") to be scoped to particular views, making it easier in some tools to, for example, select a different list item or open a menu without needing to cancel changes in an editor.
- **Debugging utilities:** Adds [#haltOnceIf:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28MessageTrace%20openMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Object%20haltOnceIf%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Implementors%20of%20haltOnceIf%3A%27%27%29%20model%5Cr%20addParentMessages%3A%20%7B%28ObjectTest%3E%3E%23testHaltOnceIf%29%20asCodeReference%7D%20autoSelectString%3A%20%23haltOnceIf%3A.%5Crnil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (MessageTrace openMessageList: #('Object haltOnceIf:') name: 'Implementors of haltOnceIf:') model  addParentMessages: {(ObjectTest>>#testHaltOnceIf) asCodeReference} autoSelectString: #haltOnceIf:. nil") as a union of [#haltOnce](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28MessageTrace%20openMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Object%20haltOnceIf%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Implementors%20of%20haltOnce%27%27%29%20model%5Cr%20addParentMessages%3A%20%7B%28ObjectTest%3E%3E%23testHaltOnceIf%29%20asCodeReference%7D%20autoSelectString%3A%20%23haltOnce.%5Crnil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (MessageTrace openMessageList: #('Object haltOnceIf:') name: 'Implementors of haltOnce') model  addParentMessages: {(ObjectTest>>#testHaltOnceIf) asCodeReference} autoSelectString: #haltOnce. nil") and [#haltIf:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28MessageTrace%20openMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Object%20haltOnceIf%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Implementors%20of%20haltIf%3A%27%27%29%20model%5Cr%20addParentMessages%3A%20%7B%28ObjectTest%3E%3E%23testHaltOnceIf%29%20asCodeReference%7D%20autoSelectString%3A%20%23haltIf%3A.%5Crnil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (MessageTrace openMessageList: #('Object haltOnceIf:') name: 'Implementors of haltIf:') model  addParentMessages: {(ObjectTest>>#testHaltOnceIf) asCodeReference} autoSelectString: #haltIf:. nil"). Makes [ObjectTracer](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseHierarchy%3A%20ObjectTracer%20selector%3A%20%23mustBeBoolean%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseHierarchy: ObjectTracer selector: #mustBeBoolean")/[ObjectViewer](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseHierarchy%3A%20ObjectViewer%20selector%3A%20%23doesNotUnderstand%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseHierarchy: ObjectViewer selector: #doesNotUnderstand:") capable of proxying true/false.
- **Debugging & simulation:**
  
  - Fixes various edge cases of the [**context simulator**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Context%3E%3E%23step%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Context>>#step"), which is used for stepping methods in the debugger. This improves the overall robustness and correctness when debugging the entire system from within itself. Concretely, we revised debugging of [Objects as Methods](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28Process%20forBlock%3A%20%5B2%20withArgs%3A%20%7B3.%204%7D%20executeMethod%3A%20%28TestObjectForMethod%20new%20xxxMethod%3A%20thisContext%20homeMethod%29%5D%29%20debug%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Process forBlock: [2 withArgs: {3. 4} executeMethod: (TestObjectForMethod new xxxMethod: thisContext homeMethod)]) debug") and [#mustBeBoolean](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Crself%20halt%3A%20%27%27Debug%20this%20and%20step%20until%20the%20DoIt%20is%20about%20to%20return%21%20%5CrWhat%20will%20%23ifTrue%3AifFalse%3A%20return%20do%20when%20sent%20to%20a%20non-boolean%20object%3F%27%27.%5Crx%20%3A%3D%20%27%27not%20a%20boolean%27%27%20ifTrue%3A%20%5B%27%27it%27%27%27%27s%20true-ish%27%27%5D%20ifFalse%3A%20%5B%27%27it%27%27%27%27s%20false-ish%27%27%5D.%5Crx%20inspect%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self halt: 'Debug this and step until the DoIt is about to return!  What will #ifTrue:ifFalse: return do when sent to a non-boolean object?'. x := 'not a boolean' ifTrue: ['it''s true-ish'] ifFalse: ['it''s false-ish']. x inspect"), corrected simulation of a few [SistaV1 instruction types](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FInstructionStream%3E%3E%23interpretSistaV1Jump%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://InstructionStream>>#interpretSistaV1Jump"), and fixed the [simulation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseVersionsOf%3A%20Context%20selector%3A%20%23doPrimitive%3Amethod%3Areceiver%3Aargs%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseVersionsOf: Context selector: #doPrimitive:method:receiver:args:") of [various](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%23%28%27%27perform%20primitives%20%2883%2C%2084%2C%20100%29%20for%20non-symbol%20selectors%27%27%20%27%27proper%20error%20codes%20for%20primitiveDoPrimitiveWithArgs%20%28118%29%27%27%20%27%27primitiveExecuteMethodArgsArray%20%28188%29%20for%20primitive%20methods%27%27%20%27%27previously%20missing%20simulation%20for%20primitiveExecuteMethod%20%28189%29%27%27%29%20exploreWithLabel%3A%20%27%27primitive%20simulation%20fixes%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// #('perform primitives (83, 84, 100) for non-symbol selectors' 'proper error codes for primitiveDoPrimitiveWithArgs (118)' 'primitiveExecuteMethodArgsArray (188) for primitive methods' 'previously missing simulation for primitiveExecuteMethod (189)') exploreWithLabel: 'primitive simulation fixes'") [control primitives](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5CrToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20ContextTest%20category%3A%20%27%27tests%20-%20simulation%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseClass: ContextTest category: 'tests - simulation'").
  - Adds support for [**recursive process-faithful debugging**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Process%3E%3E%23effectiveProcess%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Process>>#effectiveProcess"), thus making it possible not only to [debug a debugger](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Crdebugger%20%3A%3D%20%28Process%20forBlock%3A%20%5BProcessor%20activeProcess%20isActiveProcess%5D%29%20debugWithTitle%3A%20%27%27Debugger%27%27.%5Cr%28Process%20forBlock%3A%20%5Bdebugger%20stepInto%5D%29%20debugWithTitle%3A%20%27%27Debugger%20on%20debugger%27%27.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// debugger := (Process forBlock: [Processor activeProcess isActiveProcess]) debugWithTitle: 'Debugger'. (Process forBlock: [debugger stepInto]) debugWithTitle: 'Debugger on debugger'.") but also to [debug a debugger which is debugging a third debugger](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Crdebugger%20%3A%3D%20%28Process%20forBlock%3A%20%5BProcessor%20activeProcess%20isActiveProcess%5D%29%20debugWithTitle%3A%20%27%27Debugger%27%27.%5Crdebugger2%20%3A%3D%20%28Process%20forBlock%3A%20%5Bdebugger%20stepInto%5D%29%20debugWithTitle%3A%20%27%27Debugger%20on%20debugger%27%27.%5Cr%28Process%20forBlock%3A%20%5Bdebugger%20stepOver%5D%29%20debugWithTitle%3A%20%27%27Debugger%20on%20debugger%20on%20debugger%27%27.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// debugger := (Process forBlock: [Processor activeProcess isActiveProcess]) debugWithTitle: 'Debugger'. debugger2 := (Process forBlock: [debugger stepInto]) debugWithTitle: 'Debugger on debugger'. (Process forBlock: [debugger stepOver]) debugWithTitle: 'Debugger on debugger on debugger'.").
  - Signals a new [SimulationSideEffectWarning](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSimulationSideEffectWarning%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SimulationSideEffectWarning") before simulating certain primitives to allow clients such as debuggers to control the scope of simulation.
  - Fixes the debugger when stepping through a [dead home context](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%7C%20p%20%7C%5Crp%20%3A%3D%20Process%20forContext%3A%20%28%5B%5B%5D%20ensure%3A%20%5B%5D%5D%20asContext%29%20priority%3A%2040.%5Crp%20debug.%20%27%27step%20through%20%23ensure%3A%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// | p | p := Process forContext: ([[] ensure: []] asContext) priority: 40. p debug. 'step through #ensure:'").
- **Processes & scheduling:**
  
  - Improves and better documents [process termination](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcess%3E%3E%23terminate%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Process>>#terminate") and [stack unwinding](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FContext%3E%3E%23unwindAndResume%3Aevaluating%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Context>>#unwindAndResume:evaluating:") for several edge cases, making [repeated termination](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20ProcessTest%20category%3A%20%27%27tests%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseClass: ProcessTest category: 'tests'") of halfway-unwound processes and debugging in concurrent settings more reliable. Improves stepping and closing a debugger while the debugged process is [suspended](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Kernel-jar.1500%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Kernel-jar.1500'"), [blocked](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Kernel-jar.1501%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Kernel-jar.1501'"), performing a [non-local return](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Kernel-jar.1503%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Kernel-jar.1503'"), or even about to [resume](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Kernel-jar.1539%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Kernel-jar.1539'") from a BlockCannotReturn exception.
  - Prevents [Process&gt;&gt;destroy](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Process%3E%3E%23destroy%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Process>>#destroy") from crashing the entire VM when sent to a blocked process.
  - Fixes rescheduling after [BlockClosure&gt;&gt;valueAt:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBlockClosure%3E%3E%23valueAt%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://BlockClosure>>#valueAt:") and [BlockClosure&gt;&gt;valueUnpreemptively](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBlockClosure%3E%3E%23valueUnpreemptively%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://BlockClosure>>#valueUnpreemptively") by introducing a new primitive for [Process&gt;&gt;priority:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcess%3E%3E%23priority%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Process>>#priority:").
  - For threaded VMs, adds [Process&gt;&gt;isInExternalCode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcess%3E%3E%23isInExternalCode%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Process>>#isInExternalCode") to test whether a primitive is running in the background.
  - Makes it possible to [reset](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Delay%3E%3E%23reset%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Delay>>#reset") a scheduled Delay.
- **Promises:**
  
  - New [helpers for combining promises](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20Promise%20class%20category%3A%20%27%27waiting%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseClass: Promise class category: 'waiting'") such as #all:, #anyOne:, and #race:.
  - Adds and revises accessors for [awaiting](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20Promise%20category%3A%20%27%27waiting%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseClass: Promise category: 'waiting'") and [testing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20Promise%20category%3A%20%27%27testing%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseClass: Promise category: 'testing'") promises. [Promise&gt;&gt;value](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Promise%3E%3E%23value%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Promise>>#value") is now a synonym of [#wait](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Promise%3E%3E%23wait%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Promise>>#wait"); if the promise rejects, both no longer wrap the error into a BrokenPromise but re-raise it directly (see **Major deprecations** below). Accelerates waiting for already resolved promises.
  - Does not prematurely fail a promise when its resolution [triggers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Project%20uiManager%20edit%3A%20%27%27World%20future%20halt%20then%3A%20%23inspect%27%27%20label%3A%20%27%27Example%20with%20breakpoint%20in%20promise%20chain%27%27%20shouldStyle%3A%20true%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Project uiManager edit: 'World future halt then: #inspect' label: 'Example with breakpoint in promise chain' shouldStyle: true") a breakpoint or signals a (resumable) [Notification](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Notification%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Notification").
- **Methods, context, and block closures:**
  
  - Adjusts further accessors and queries for the new SistaV1 bytecode set and full block closures. Cleans up vocabulary on BlockClosure and Context for accessing [home contexts](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Context%3E%3E%23home%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Context>>#home") and [methods](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20FullBlockClosure%3E%3E%23homeMethod%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// FullBlockClosure>>#homeMethod").
  - For Sista, allows creating classes with up to [64k instance variables](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Behavior%3E%3E%23maxInstSize%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Behavior>>#maxInstSize").
  - Fixes different [instruction tests](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%7B%27%27CompiledCode%20hasInstVarRef%27%27.%20%27%27CompiledCode%20hasMethodReturn%27%27%7D%20name%3A%20%27%27Fixed%20instruction%20tests%20for%20Sista%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: {'CompiledCode hasInstVarRef'. 'CompiledCode hasMethodReturn'} name: 'Fixed instruction tests for Sista'").
  - Speeds up [bytecode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27CompiledCode%20sendsToSuper%27%27%20%27%27CompiledMethod%20readsField%3A%27%27%20%27%27CompiledMethod%20writesField%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Accelerated%20literal%20enumeration%20methods%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('CompiledCode sendsToSuper' 'CompiledMethod readsField:' 'CompiledMethod writesField:') name: 'Accelerated literal enumeration methods'")/[literal](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20CompiledCode%3E%3E%23allLiteralsDo%3AscanForSpecial%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// CompiledCode>>#allLiteralsDo:scanForSpecial:") enumeration for faster SystemNavigation queries.
  - Improves method symbolic text through [colorful styling](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28CompiledCode%3E%3E%23symbolicText%29%20browse%20toggleShowingByteCodes%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (CompiledCode>>#symbolicText) browse toggleShowingByteCodes") and by labeling [instance variable references](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28Context%3E%3E%23isClosureContext%29%20browse%20toggleShowingByteCodes%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Context>>#isClosureContext) browse toggleShowingByteCodes").
  - Makes [CompiledCode equality](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27CompiledCode%20%3D%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'CompiledCode ='; actOnClickFor: Model new") invariant under the caches used for [internationalization](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20String%3E%3E%23translated%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// String>>#translated") and [BlockClosure&gt;&gt;once](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBlockClosure%3E%3E%23once%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://BlockClosure>>#once"). Improves [parsing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20CompiledMethod%3E%3E%23dateMethodLastSubmitted%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// CompiledMethod>>#dateMethodLastSubmitted") of method timestamps.
  - Allows constructing [MessageSends](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20MessageSend%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// MessageSend") without a receiver and extends their accessing/conversion/testing protocols. Improves polymorphism with [WeakMessageSend](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWeakMessageSend%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WeakMessageSend").
  - Speeds up enumeration of [pragmas](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20CompiledMethod%3E%3E%23pragmasDo%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// CompiledMethod>>#pragmasDo:") and [selectors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20CompiledMethod%3E%3E%23anyAndAllSelectorsDo%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// CompiledMethod>>#anyAndAllSelectorsDo:") by 20%.
- **Classes and organizers:**
  
  - Speeds up [class (re-)building](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ClassBuilder%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ClassBuilder") by more than 30% by using the MethodMassage package (see below).
  - Improves overall reliability of [source logging](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%5BChangeList%20browseRecentLog%5D%20valueSupplyingAnswer%3A%201.%20nil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// [ChangeList browseRecentLog] valueSupplyingAnswer: 1. nil") after correcting a syntax error and when recategorizing classes, removing class variables, or changing class comments.
  - Automatically [sanitizes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20PackageInfo%20class%3E%3E%23sanitizeName%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// PackageInfo class>>#sanitizeName:") category and package names by removing leading/trailing spaces. Adds conveniences [Categorizer&gt;&gt;includesCategory:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Categorizer%3E%3E%23includesCategory%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Categorizer>>#includesCategory:") and [#classifyAll:under:suppressIfDefault:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Categorizer%3E%3E%23classifyAll%3Aunder%3AsuppressIfDefault%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Categorizer>>#classifyAll:under:suppressIfDefault:"). Renames [ClassDescription&gt;&gt;](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ClassDescription%3E%3E%23allMethodsInCategory%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://
