"code:// ClassDescription>>#selectorsInCategory:").
- Persists the preferences [processPreemptionYields](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SmalltalkImage%3E%3E%23processPreemptionYields%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SmalltalkImage>>#processPreemptionYields:") and ["map control keys to command keys"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20EventSensor%20getter%3A%20%23mapControlKeysToCommandKeys%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: EventSensor getter: #mapControlKeysToCommandKeys) open") across image restarts.
- Improves multilingual support in various places.
- Miscellaneous improvements to documentation of [suspension/resumption points](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27Process%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'Process Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new"), [stack unwinding](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5CrToolSet%20browseMessageNames%3A%20%27%27unwindTo%3A%27%27%20%2C%20%28Character%20value%3A%2059%29%20%2C%20%27%27unwindTo%3Asafely%3A%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMessageNames: 'unwindTo:' , (Character value: 59) , 'unwindTo:safely:'"), and others.
- Miscellaneous improvements to test coverage, especially for [simulation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FContextTest%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ContextTest"), [unwinding](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcessUnwindTest%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ProcessUnwindTest"), and [process termination](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProcessTest%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ProcessTest").
- Overall clean-ups and refactorings. Work continues on improving modularization and avoiding [dependencies](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDependencyBrowser%20openOnPackage%3A%20Object%20packageInfo%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DependencyBrowser openOnPackage: Object packageInfo") from core packages on ST80, MorphicExtras, and EToys.

*Collections (up to* Collections-ct.1115/CollectionsTests-ct.419*)*

- Adds many new conveniences for enumerating, copying, and modifying [**collections**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Crself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Collection%20withoutDuplicates%27%27%20%27%27Collection%20copyWithoutDuplicates%27%27%20%27%27SequenceableCollection%20findAll%3A%27%27%20%27%27SequenceableCollection%20findFirst%3AstartingAt%3A%27%27%20%27%27SequenceableCollection%20groupsCollect%3A%27%27%20%27%27SequenceableCollection%20subsequences%3A%27%27%20%27%27SequenceableCollection%20with%3Aselect%3A%27%27%20%27%27SequenceableCollection%20withIndexSelect%3A%27%27%20%27%27RawBitsArray%20byteAt%3A%27%27%20%27%27RawBitsArray%20byteAt%3Aput%3A%27%27%20%27%27RawBitsArray%20reverseEndianness%27%27%20%27%27ByteArray%20class%20newCryptographicallyRandom%3A%27%27%20%27%27RunArray%20intervalsAndValuesDo%3A%27%27%20%27%27Dictionary%20with%3Ado%3A%27%27%20%27%27Dictionary%20with%3Acollect%3A%27%27%20%27%27Dictionary%20withKeysValuesAnd%3Ado%3A%27%27%20%27%27Dictionary%20withKeysValuesAnd%3Acollect%3A%27%27%20%27%27Matrix%20%40%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27New%20methods%20in%20collection%20hierarchy%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('Collection withoutDuplicates' 'Collection copyWithoutDuplicates' 'SequenceableCollection findAll:' 'SequenceableCollection findFirst:startingAt:' 'SequenceableCollection groupsCollect:' 'SequenceableCollection subsequences:' 'SequenceableCollection with:select:' 'SequenceableCollection withIndexSelect:' 'RawBitsArray byteAt:' 'RawBitsArray byteAt:put:' 'RawBitsArray reverseEndianness' 'ByteArray class newCryptographicallyRandom:' 'RunArray intervalsAndValuesDo:' 'Dictionary with:do:' 'Dictionary with:collect:' 'Dictionary withKeysValuesAnd:do:' 'Dictionary withKeysValuesAnd:collect:' 'Matrix @') name: 'New methods in collection hierarchy'") **and** [**streams**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Crself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27PositionableStream%20peek%3A%27%27%20%27%27Stream%20isTextStream%27%27%20%27%27SharedQueue2%20atEnd%27%27%20%27%27SharedQueue2%20nextTimeout%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27New%20methods%20in%20Stream%20hierarchy%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('PositionableStream peek:' 'Stream isTextStream' 'SharedQueue2 atEnd' 'SharedQueue2 nextTimeout:') name: 'New methods in Stream hierarchy'") (click to browse them all!). Improves [**weak typing**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Collection%3E%3E%23adaptToCollection%3AandSend%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Collection>>#adaptToCollection:andSend:") between magnitudes and dictionaries so that arithmetic can be applied to dictionaries ([click here for examples!](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20openWorkspaceExample%3A%20%23examplesDictionariesWeakTyping%20title%3A%20%27%27Weak%20typing%20with%20dictionaries%27%27%20shouldStyle%3A%20true%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes openWorkspaceExample: #examplesDictionariesWeakTyping title: 'Weak typing with dictionaries' shouldStyle: true")).
- Introduces [AlternativeSortFunction](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMessageNames%3A%20%27%27sortedFirst%3BsortedLast%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMessageNames: 'sortedFirst;sortedLast'") for sorting collections based on binary predicates.
- Adds new **string conveniences** for [prefix/suffix testing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMessageNames%3A%20%27%27beginsWith*%27%27%20%2C%20%28Character%20value%3A%2059%29%20%2C%20%27%27endsWith*%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMessageNames: 'beginsWith*' , (Character value: 59) , 'endsWith*'"), [escaping](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27String%20escapeQuote%3A%27%27%20%27%27String%20unescapeQuote%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27New%20string%20methods%20for%20escaping%2Funescaping%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('String escapeQuote:' 'String unescapeQuote:') name: 'New string methods for escaping/unescaping'"), and [index-to-row/column conversion](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOfList%3A%20%23%28indexCorrespondingToLine%3Acharacter%3A%20indicesCorrespondingToLine%3A%20lineCorrespondingToIndex%3A%29%20title%3A%20%27%27Methods%20for%20index-to-row%2Fcol%20conversion%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOfList: #(indexCorrespondingToLine:character: indicesCorrespondingToLine: lineCorrespondingToIndex:) title: 'Methods for index-to-row/col conversion'").
- Improves printStrings and object explorer representations of [bags](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%28Collection%20packageInfo%20classes%20collect%3A%20%23superclass%20as%3A%20Bag%29%20inspect%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Collection packageInfo classes collect: #superclass as: Bag) inspect") and [sets](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28Collection%20packageInfo%20classes%20collect%3A%20%23superclass%20as%3A%20Set%29%20explore%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Collection packageInfo classes collect: #superclass as: Set) explore"), [dictionaries](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28HashedCollection%20withAllSubclasses%20collect%3A%20%5B%3Aea%20%7C%20ea%20-%3E%20ea%20superclass%5D%20as%3A%20Dictionary%29%20inspect%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(HashedCollection withAllSubclasses collect: [:ea | ea -> ea superclass] as: Dictionary) inspect") and [run arrays](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28%23%28one%20three%20three%20three%20two%20two%20three%20three%20three%29%20as%3A%20RunArray%29%20explore%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(#(one three three three two two three three three) as: RunArray) explore"), [strings](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%27%27i%20%3C3%20squeak%27%27%20explore%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://'i <3 squeak' explore") and [characters](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%24S%20explore%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://$S explore"). Adds [preview of formatted texts](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28Inspector%20openOn%3A%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20v61%20contents%29%20model%20selectFieldNamed%3A%20%27%27text%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Inspector openOn: SqueakReleaseNotes v61 contents) model selectFieldNamed: 'text'") to object inspectors.
- **Texts:** Adds [#addAttributeBack:\*](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28SystemBrowser%20default%20fullOnClass%3A%20Text%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27back%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (SystemBrowser default fullOnClass: Text) filterMessages: 'back'") for adding new attributes beneath existing attributes and fixes [TextStream&gt;&gt;withAttribute](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextStream%3E%3E%23withAttribute%3Ado%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextStream>>#withAttribute:do:")\[[s](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextStream%3E%3E%23withAttributes%3Ado%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextStream>>#withAttributes:do:")][:do:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextStream%3E%3E%23withAttribute%3Ado%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextStream>>#withAttribute:do:") to preserve existing dominating attributes. Adds conveniences for [testing texts for attributes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20Text%20category%3A%20%27%27testing%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseClass: Text category: 'testing'") and [converting them to plain text](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23asPlainText%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #asPlainText"). Improves [polymorphism](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Text%20class%20empty%27%27%20%27%27Text%20findString%3A%27%27%20%27%27Text%20lines%27%27%20%27%27Text%20withoutTrailingBlanks%27%27%20%27%27Text%20sameAs%3A%27%27%20%27%27Text%20withBlanksTrimmed%27%27%20%27%27Text%20withoutLeadingBlanks%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27String%20messages%20that%20are%20now%20also%20understood%20by%20texts%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('Text class empty' 'Text findString:' 'Text lines' 'Text withoutTrailingBlanks' 'Text sameAs:' 'Text withBlanksTrimmed' 'Text withoutLeadingBlanks') name: 'String messages that are now also understood by texts'") of texts with strings.
- Improves **HTML parsing** so that it now can correctly process most clipboard contents and rich-text emails (see below):
  
  - Makes it possible to [block downloads or execution](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20HtmlReadWriter%3E%3E%23defaultPermissions%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// HtmlReadWriter>>#defaultPermissions") of untrusted HTML resources.
  - Adds [support for &lt;blockquote&gt;](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FHtmlReadWriter%3E%3E%23mapBlockquoteTag%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://HtmlReadWriter>>#mapBlockquoteTag").
  - Revises [whitespace handling](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FHtmlReadWriter%3E%3E%23breakLines%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://HtmlReadWriter>>#breakLines:") for tags such as &lt;p&gt;, &lt;div&gt;, and &lt;pre&gt;.
  - Properly ignores [non-textual elements](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20HtmlReadWriter%3E%3E%23excludedTags%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// HtmlReadWriter>>#excludedTags") such as &lt;script&gt; and &lt;style&gt; tags.
  - Improves robustness for parsing [HTML5 void tags](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FHtmlReadWriter%3E%3E%23voidTags%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://HtmlReadWriter>>#voidTags"), redundant closing tags, and [invalid/unsupported CSS properties](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FHtmlReadWriterTest%3E%3E%23test13SpanTag%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://HtmlReadWriterTest>>#test13SpanTag").
  - Fixes escaping/unescaping of special characters in [HTML/XML entities](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27String%20escapeEntities%27%27%20%27%27String%20asHtml%27%27%20%27%27String%20asUnHtml%27%27%20%27%27HtmlReadWriter%20htmlEscape%3A%27%27%20%27%27HtmlReadWriter%20writeContent%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Fixed%20HTML%2FXML%20entities%20%28un%29escaping%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('String escapeEntities' 'String asHtml' 'String asUnHtml' 'HtmlReadWriter htmlEscape:' 'HtmlReadWriter writeContent:') name: 'Fixed HTML/XML entities (un)escaping'") and [URLs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20TextURL%3E%3E%23openHtmlOn%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: TextURL>>#openHtmlOn:").
- Adds support for [base32 encoding/decoding](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMessageNames%3A%20%27%27base32%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMessageNames: 'base32'").
- Adds [Object&gt;&gt;isAssociation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Object%3E%3E%23isAssociation%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Object>>#isAssociation").
- Miscellaneous performance improvements for [sequenceable collection accessing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SequenceableCollection%3E%3E%23atAllPut%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SequenceableCollection>>#atAllPut:"), [linked list enumeration](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FLinkedList%3E%3E%23with%3Acollect%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://LinkedList>>#with:collect:"), RunArray [accessing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRunArray%3E%3E%23findAll%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RunArray>>#findAll:") and [sorting](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20RunArray%20category%3A%20%27%27sorting%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseClass: RunArray category: 'sorting'"), [string substitution](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28MCRepository%20trunk%20versionNamed%3A%20%27%27Collections-ul.1102.mcz%27%27%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (MCRepository trunk versionNamed: 'Collections-ul.1102.mcz') open") and [tokenization](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20String%3E%3E%23substrings%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// String>>#substrings"), [text formatting](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Text%20addAllAttributes%3A%27%27%20%27%27Text%20addAllAttributes%3Afrom%3Ato%3A%27%27%20%27%27TextStream%20withAttributes%3Ado%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Accelerated%20text%20formatting%20methods%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('Text addAllAttributes:' 'Text addAllAttributes:from:to:' 'TextStream withAttributes:do:') name: 'Accelerated text formatting methods'"), and [character printing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FCharacter%3E%3E%23printOn%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Character>>#printOn:").
- Several bug fixes for [RawBitsArray accessing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20RawBitsArray%3E%3E%23byteAt%3Aput%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: RawBitsArray>>#byteAt:put:"), [matrix enumeration](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20Matrix%3E%3E%23with%3Ado%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: Matrix>>#with:do:"), [stream resetting](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27ReadStream%20reset%27%27%20%27%27WriteStream%20reset%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Fixed%20stream%20resetting%20methods%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('ReadStream reset' 'WriteStream reset') name: 'Fixed stream resetting methods'") and [binary streaming](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20PositionableStream%3E%3E%23isBinary%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: PositionableStream>>#isBinary"), and [filing in of hashed collections](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20HashedCollection%3E%3E%23comeFullyUpOnReload%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: HashedCollection>>#comeFullyUpOnReload:"). Enhances accuracy of [String&gt;&gt;findSelector heuristics](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20String%3E%3E%23findSelector%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: String>>#findSelector") (used by \[CMD] + \[N] for "senders of it" et al.) by trying to reuse the actual Smalltalk parser first. Improves hashing, copying, and comparing of [text attributes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextAttribute%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextAttribute") and [sort functions](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSortFunction%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SortFunction").
- Improves multilingual support in several places. Switches [#asCommaStringAnd](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Collection%3E%3E%23asCommaStringAnd%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Collection>>#asCommaStringAnd") to use the Oxford comma.
- Overall clean-ups, refactorings, and [documentation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27WeakIdentityDictionary%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'WeakIdentityDictionary Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new") [improvements](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27Text%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'Text Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new").

*Chronology (up to* Chronology-Core-ct.98/Chronology-Tests-ct.40*)*

- [**Refactors time zones**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTimeZone%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TimeZone") [into an extensible class hierarchy](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTimeZone%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TimeZone") to enable the definition of DST-aware zones such as [TZ-Olson](https://wiki.squeak.org/squeak/5644).
- Restores ANSI compliance of [DateAndTime&gt;&gt;offset:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDateAndTime%3E%3E%23offset%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DateAndTime>>#offset:") by answering an equivalent time instead of reinterpreting it in a different time zone. Adds [#asLocalAt:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDateAndTime%3E%3E%23asLocalAt%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DateAndTime>>#asLocalAt:") and [#utcOffset:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDateAndTime%3E%3E%23utcOffset%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DateAndTime>>#utcOffset:") as compatibility synonyms of the old and new behavior.
- Repairs ["high-resolution clock for timing" preference](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20Time%20getter%3A%20%23useHighResClockForTiming%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: Time getter: #useHighResClockForTiming) open") and improves precision of [Time class&gt;&gt;nanosecondsToRunHighRes:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTime%20class%3E%3E%23nanosecondsToRunHighRes%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Time class>>#nanosecondsToRunHighRes:") when the preference is enabled.
- Fixes a [race condition](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTestValueWithinFix%3E%3E%23testValueWithinTermination%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TestValueWithinFix>>#testValueWithinTermination") when terminating a process during [BlockClosure&gt;&gt;valueWithin:onTimeout:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBlockClosure%3E%3E%23valueWithin%3AonTimeout%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://BlockClosure>>#valueWithin:onTimeout:").
- Overall improvements to [documentation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27DateAndTime%20asUTC%27%27%20%27%27DateAndTime%20makeUTC%27%27%20%27%27Timespan%20beCanonical%27%27%20%27%27TimeZone%20Comment%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Improved%20Chronology%20documentation%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('DateAndTime asUTC' 'DateAndTime makeUTC' 'Timespan beCanonical' 'TimeZone Comment') name: 'Improved Chronology documentation'"). Adds [convenient](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28Inspector%20openOn%3A%20Date%20today%29%20model%20selectFieldNamed%3A%20%27%27month%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Inspector openOn: Date today) model selectFieldNamed: 'month'") [accessor](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Inspector%20openOn%3A%20Time%20now%29%20model%20selectFieldNamed%3A%20%27%27minute%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Inspector openOn: Time now) model selectFieldNamed: 'minute'") [fields](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDateAndTime%20today%20asWeek%20inspect%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DateAndTime today asWeek inspect") to inspectors on chronology objects.

*Compiler (up to* Compiler-ct.535*)*

- Allows [**sending #pvt* selectors to super**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openWorkspaceExample%3A%20%23examplesSendingPvtToSuper%20title%3A%20%27%27Sending%20pvt*%20to%20super%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openWorkspaceExample: #examplesSendingPvtToSuper title: 'Sending pvt* to super'"), thus making it possible to override and extend private methods in subclasses.
- **Parser warnings:**
  
  - Adds a new opt-in preference for [interactive warnings about variable shadowing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20ShadowedVariableNotification%20getter%3A%20%23enableInteractiveWarning%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: ShadowedVariableNotification getter: #enableInteractiveWarning) open") to replace classical transcript warnings.
  - Removes warnings about [undefined temporary variables](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUndefinedVariable%3E%3E%23openMenuIn%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UndefinedVariable>>#openMenuIn:") when they are being sent a [nil check](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FParser%3E%3E%23primaryExpression%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Parser>>#primaryExpression").
  - Adds missing warning about [unknown binary selectors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Project%20uiManager%20edit%3A%20%27%271%20%C2%A7%C2%A7%C2%A7%C2%A7%20222%27%27%20label%3A%20%27%27Example%27%27%20shouldStyle%3A%20true%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Project uiManager edit: '1 §§§§ 222' label: 'Example' shouldStyle: true"), analogously to warnings about unknown unary and keyword selectors.
- **Improves handling of comments** in the parse tree by always assigning them to the nearest expression or statement and printing nodes with comments more reliably. Adds new preference to control [hard-wrapping comments during pretty-printing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20ParseNode%20getter%3A%20%23prefPrettyPrintCommentWidthInPoints%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: ParseNode getter: #prefPrettyPrintCommentWidthInPoints) open"), which is now disabled by default.
- **Records** [**source ranges**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMethodNode%3E%3E%23rawSourceRanges%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MethodNode>>#rawSourceRanges") **for variables** by introducing [VariableUsageNode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FVariableUsageNode%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://VariableUsageNode") to benefit selection in the debugger and source localization for other AST users.
- Avoids [side-effects on symbol](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSymbol%20class%3E%3E%23intern%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Symbol class>>#intern:") [tables](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSymbol%20class%3E%3E%23intern%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Symbol class>>#intern:") during parsing by signaling new [UnknownSymbolNotification](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUnknownSymbolNotification%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UnknownSymbolNotification") that cautious clients can handle before a new symbol is created.
- Moves Compiler class&gt;&gt;[parserClass](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20%5Cr%20methodHierarchyBrowserForClass%3A%20Behavior%5Cr%20selector%3A%20%23parserClass%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation   methodHierarchyBrowserForClass: Behavior  selector: #parserClass") to [Compiler class&gt;&gt;preferredParserClass](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20%5Cr%20methodHierarchyBrowserForClass%3A%20Compiler%5Cr%20selector%3A%20%23preferredParserClass%5Cr%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation   methodHierarchyBrowserForClass: Compiler  selector: #preferredParserClass ").
- Fixes several parser bugs regarding pragmas ([pragmas before temporaries](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FParser%3E%3E%23parseArgsAndTemps%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Parser>>#parseArgsAndTemps:"), [unary pragma parsers](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FParser%3E%3E%23simulationGuard%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Parser>>#simulationGuard")), [handling underscore selectors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FParser%3E%3E%23parseParameterNames%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Parser>>#parseParameterNames:"), parsing [standalone literals](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FString%3E%3E%23findLiteral%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://String>>#findLiteral"), and [compiling future sends](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28MCRepository%20trunk%20versionNamed%3A%20%27%27Compiler-pre.493.mcz%27%27%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(MCRepository trunk versionNamed: 'Compiler-pre.493.mcz') open"). Corrects slips during encoding and decompiling of [Sista-specific](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FEncoderForSistaV1%20class%3E%3E%23isReturnTopFromMethodAt%3Ain%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://EncoderForSistaV1 class>>#isReturnTopFromMethodAt:in:") [instructions](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28MCRepository%20trunk%20versionNamed%3A%20%27%27Compiler-eem.483.mcz%27%27%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (MCRepository trunk versionNamed: 'Compiler-eem.483.mcz') open") and [full blocks](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDecompiler%3E%3E%23pushFullClosure%3AnumCopied%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Decompiler>>#pushFullClosure:numCopied:").
- Adds a [utility](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20BytecodeEncoder%20class%3E%3E%23condensedFrequencyTable%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// BytecodeEncoder class>>#condensedFrequencyTable") to compute static bytecode frequencies.
- Miscellaneous [documentation improvements](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Crself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27EncoderForV3%20class%20isSendAt%3Ain%3A%27%27%20%27%27EncoderForSistaV1%20Definition%27%27%20%27%27BraceNode%20Comment%27%27%20%5Cr%27%27CompilationCue%20Comment%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Documentation%20improvements%20in%20Compiler%20package%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('EncoderForV3 class isSendAt:in:' 'EncoderForSistaV1 Definition' 'BraceNode Comment'  'CompilationCue Comment') name: 'Documentation improvements in Compiler package'").

*Environments (up to* Environments-ct.88*)*

- Adds [Object&gt;&gt;isEnvironment](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FObject%3E%3E%23isEnvironment%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Object>>#isEnvironment").
- Speeds up [Environment&gt;&gt;classOrTraitNamed:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FEnvironment%3E%3E%23classOrTraitNamed%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Environment>>#classOrTraitNamed:").

*PackageInfo (up to* PackageInfo-ct.81*)*

- Automatically [sanitizes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20PackageInfo%20class%3E%3E%23sanitizeName%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// PackageInfo class>>#sanitizeName:") package names by removing leading/trailing spaces.
- Improves [multi-environment support](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27PackageInfo-Base-ct.81%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'PackageInfo-Base-ct.81'") when accessing classes of a package.
- Accelerates [package-from-method lookup](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20PackageOrganizer%3E%3E%23packageOfMethodCategory%3AofClass%3AifNone%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: PackageOrganizer>>#packageOfMethodCategory:ofClass:ifNone:"), speeding up Monticello snapshots by up to 400%.

*Traits (up to* Traits-ct.320*)*

- Adds [trait templates with composition](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FClassDescription%20class%3E%3E%23newTraitTemplateIn%3Acomposition%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ClassDescription class>>#newTraitTemplateIn:composition:").
- Improves [multi-environment support](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Traits-ct.320%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Traits-ct.320'"), so one can define and access traits in other environments correctly.
- Minor refactorings, in particular, [uses](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllCallsOn%3A%20%23AssemblerMethod%20localToPackage%3A%20Trait%20packageInfo%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseAllCallsOn: #AssemblerMethod localToPackage: Trait packageInfo") new MethodMassage package for compiling traits.

*MethodMassage (up to* MethodMassage-ct.75*)*  
Welcomes the [**MethodMassage package**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAssemblerMethod%20packageInfo%20browse%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AssemblerMethod packageInfo browse") in the trunk, which makes it convenient to [disassemble](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FCompiledMethod%3E%3E%23disassemble%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://CompiledMethod>>#disassemble") compiled methods as [first-class objects](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAssemblerMethod%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AssemblerMethod") with [reified labels](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%7C%20tree%20%7C%5Crtree%20%3A%3D%20%28%28ToolSet%20explore%3A%20%28AssemblerMethod%3E%3E%23labelNameFor%3A%29%20disassemble%29%5Cr%20findA%3A%20PluggableTreeMorph%29%20ifNil%3A%20%5B%5Eself%5D.%5Crtree%20items%20detect%3A%20%5B%3Aea%20%7C%20%28ea%20contentsAtColumn%3A%201%29%20%3D%20%27%27instructions%27%27%5D%20ifFound%3A%20%23expand.%5Crtree%20containingWindow%20extent%3A%20500px%40300px.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// | tree | tree := ((ToolSet explore: (AssemblerMethod>>#labelNameFor:) disassemble)  findA: PluggableTreeMorph) ifNil: [^self]. tree items detect: [:ea | (ea contentsAtColumn: 1) = 'instructions'] ifFound: #expand. tree containingWindow extent: 500px@300px."), inspect and rewrite them, and [reassemble](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAssemblerMethod%3E%3E%23assemble%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AssemblerMethod>>#assemble") them. Previously, MethodMassage was developed and tested in the [VMMaker repository](https://wiki.squeak.org/squeak/vmmaker) for at least 10 years. Recent changes to the package include [instance variable remapping](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAssemblerMethod%3E%3E%23reshapeUsing%3AoldVars%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AssemblerMethod>>#reshapeUsing:oldVars:") to support class reshaping in the trunk, improved documentation and test coverage, several performance improvements, and [full support](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMethodMassageTests%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MethodMassageTests") for the V3PlusClosures bytecode set alongside SistaV1.

**~~~ Graphics & Sound ~~~**

*Graphics (up to* Graphics-nice.562*)*

- Adds support for [basic CSS color functions](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openWorkspaceExample%3A%20%23examplesCSSColors%20title%3A%20%27%27CSS%20colors%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openWorkspaceExample: #examplesCSSColors title: 'CSS colors'") to improve [HTML parsing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openWorkspaceExample%3A%20%23examplesHTMLWithCSSColors%20title%3A%20%27%27HTML%20with%20CSS%20colors%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openWorkspaceExample: #examplesHTMLWithCSSColors title: 'HTML with CSS colors'").
- Adds [dithering](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FForm%3E%3E%23dither%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Form>>#dither:") for smoothly reducing the color depth of [forms](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28%28%28SystemWindow%20windowsIn%3A%20Project%20current%20world%29%20at%3A%201%20ifAbsent%3A%20%5BWatchMorph%20new%5D%29%20imageForm%20dither%3A%204%29%20openAsMorph.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest%20%5C%22fixme%5C%22%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D 'code://(((SystemWindow windowsIn: Project current world) at: 1 ifAbsent: [WatchMorph new]) imageForm dither: 4) openAsMorph. self flag: #doNotTest "fixme"').
- Adds [accessors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20DisplayScreen%20class%20category%3A%20%27%27host%20OS%20window%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseClass: DisplayScreen class category: 'host OS window'") for screen dimensions of multiple monitors.
- Improves [font preview](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20AbstractFont%3E%3E%23symbolSample%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// AbstractFont>>#symbolSample") for symbol fonts.
- Fixes [Color mercury](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Color%20mercury%20openAsMorph%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Color mercury openAsMorph"), decoding of [transparent pixel values](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27Color%20class%20colorFromPixelValue%3Adepth%3A%27%27%20%27%27Form%20isTransparentAt%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Fixed%20pixel%20value%20decoders%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: Form>>#copyWithColorsReducedTo:") and [cursors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20CursorWithMask%3E%3E%23blankCopyOf%3AscaledBy%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// StrikeFont>>#hasGlyphForCode:"), opening of [GIF "code:// GIFReadWriter class"), and loading of translucent Removes unused font data to save space.
- Miscellaneous improvements for multilingual support, documentation, and test coverage.
- Deprecates [AnimatedGIFReadWriter](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAnimatedGIFReadWriter%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AnimatedGIFReadWriter") in favor of [GIFReadWriter](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FGIFReadWriter%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://GIFReadWriter").

*Balloon (up to* Balloon-ct.36*)*

- Minor [performance improvements](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Balloon-eem.35%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Balloon-eem.35'").
- Improves documentation of [gradient fills](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FGradientFillStyle%20class%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://GradientFillStyle class").

*TrueType (Fonts) (up to* TrueType-tpr.99*)*

- Fixes backward compatibility with fonts that only have a [deprecated Unicode mapping table](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTTCharacterMappingTable%3E%3E%23prepareFromUnicode%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TTCharacterMappingTable>>#prepareFromUnicode").
- Adds [utility](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23collectAllGlyphs%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #collectAllGlyphs") for exploring all glyphs of a font.

*Sound (up to* Sound-ct.102*)*

- Adds accessors for enumerating available [sound player](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28SystemBrowser%20default%20fullOnClass%3A%20SoundPlayer%20class%20category%3A%20%27%27accessing%27%27%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27player%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (SystemBrowser default fullOnClass: SoundPlayer class category: 'accessing') filterMessages: 'player'") and [recorder](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28SystemBrowser%20default%20fullOnClass%3A%20SoundRecorder%20class%20category%3A%20%27%27accessing%27%27%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27recorder%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (SystemBrowser default fullOnClass: SoundRecorder class category: 'accessing') filterMessages: 'recorder'") devices and setting the current device.
- Adds missing methods for [stopping composite sounds](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20%5Cr%20methodHierarchyBrowserForClass%3A%20AbstractSound%20%5Cr%20selector%3A%20%23stopGracefully%20%5Cr%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation   methodHierarchyBrowserForClass: AbstractSound   selector: #stopGracefully  ").
- Adds a [file service](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SampledSound%20class%3E%3E%23serviceLoadingAiff%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SampledSound class>>#serviceLoadingAiff") for reading AIFF files.
- Adds [convenient inspector fields](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%7C%20sound%20%7C%5Crsound%20%3A%3D%20SampledSound%20exampleBach%20play.%5Cr%28Inspector%20openOn%3A%20sound%29%5Cr%20model%20selectFieldNamed%3A%20%27%27duration%27%27.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// | sound | sound := SampledSound exampleBach play. (Inspector openOn: sound)  model selectFieldNamed: 'duration'. self flag: #doNotTest") to sounds.
- Improves reliability of [storing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAbstractSound%3E%3E%23storeSampleCount%3AbigEndian%3Aon%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AbstractSound>>#storeSampleCount:bigEndian:on:") sounds (fixes redundant trailing zeros and an endianness issue when storing on in-memory streams).
- Improves performance of [StreamingMonoSound](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FStreamingMonoSound%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://StreamingMonoSound").

**~~~ Files & Network ~~~**

*Files (up to* Files-mt.223*)*

- Adds a [preference](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20SourceFileArray%20getter%3A%20%23internalizeChanges%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: SourceFileArray getter: #internalizeChanges) open") for **caching the changes file** [**in the image**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FChangeLogStream%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ChangeLogStream") instead of logging changes to disk. This complements [existing support for caching the sources file](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20CompressedSources%20getter%3A%20%23internalizeSources%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: CompressedSources getter: #internalizeSources) open") in the image (introduced in Squeak 6.0) and allows a Squeak image to run as a standalone file without further dependencies on the file system.
- Provides access to [**system environment variables**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFileDirectory%20class%3E%3E%23getenv%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FileDirectory class>>#getenv:") and the [**current working directory**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27FileDirectory%20class%20workingDirectory%27%27%20%27%27FileDirectory%20class%20workingDirectory%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Message%20list%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('FileDirectory class workingDirectory' 'FileDirectory class workingDirectory:') name: 'Message list'") of the VM.
- On Linux/Unix platforms, adds support for [tilde (~) path expansion](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20Project%20uiManager%20edit%3A%20%27%27UnixFileDirectory%20default%20fullNameFor%3A%20%27%27%27%27%7E%2F.profile%27%27%27%27%27%27%20label%3A%20%27%27Home%20directory%20path%20extension%20example%27%27%20shouldStyle%3A%20true%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// Project uiManager edit: 'UnixFileDirectory default fullNameFor: ''~/.profile''' label: 'Home directory path extension example' shouldStyle: true") to the home directory.
- Adds a file service for [browsing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleBrowseSourcesFileChanges%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleBrowseSourcesFileChanges") `.sources` [files](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleBrowseSourcesFileChanges%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleBrowseSourcesFileChanges").
- Improves reliability of [listing directories](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20ToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20FileDirectory%3E%3E%23directoryContentsFor%3Ado%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ToolSet browseMethodVersion: FileDirectory>>#directoryContentsFor:do:") with some inaccessible entries, testing whether a [directory exists](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFileDirectory%3E%3E%23exists%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FileDirectory>>#exists"), calculating [creation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDirectoryEntry%3E%3E%23creationDateAndTime%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DirectoryEntry>>#creationDateAndTime") and [modification times](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDirectoryEntry%3E%3E%23modificationDateAndTime%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DirectoryEntry>>#modificationDateAndTime") of files.
- Improves error detection when [opening a file](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20FileStream%20class%3E%3E%23detectFile%3Ado%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: FileStream class>>#detectFile:do:") or [copying files](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20FileDirectory%3E%3E%23copyHere%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: FileDirectory>>#copyHere:").
- Fixes [filing out](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFileDirectory%3E%3E%23writeSourceCodeFrom%3AbaseName%3AisSt%3AuseHtml%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FileDirectory>>#writeSourceCodeFrom:baseName:isSt:useHtml:") of methods or [change sets](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFileDirectory%3E%3E%23nextNameFor%3Aextension%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FileDirectory>>#nextNameFor:extension:") with a slash in their name.
- Improves performance when [parsing file names](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFileDirectory%20class%3E%3E%23extensionFor%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FileDirectory class>>#extensionFor:"), [testing directory existence](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFileDirectory%3E%3E%23exists%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FileDirectory>>#exists"), and [reopening a file in read-only mode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FStandardFileStream%3E%3E%23readOnlyCopy%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://StandardFileStream>>#readOnlyCopy").
- Extends documentation on [file opening](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20FileStream%20class%20category%3A%20%27%27instance%20creation%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseClass: FileStream class category: 'instance creation'"), [long file paths on Windows](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27FileDirectory%20Comment%27%27%20%27%27FileDirectory%20primLookupEntryIn%3Aindex%3A%27%27%20%27%27FileDirectory%20primLookupEntryIn%3Aname%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Extended%20documentation%20on%20long%20Windows%20file%20paths%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('FileDirectory Comment' 'FileDirectory primLookupEntryIn:index:' 'FileDirectory primLookupEntryIn:name:') name: 'Extended documentation on long Windows file paths'"), and [creation/modification dates](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27DirectoryEntry%20creationDateAndTime%27%27%20%27%27DirectoryEntry%20modificationDateAndTime%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Updated%20documentation%20of%20file%20creation%2Fmodification%20dates%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('DirectoryEntry creationDateAndTime' 'DirectoryEntry modificationDateAndTime') name: 'Updated documentation of file creation/modification dates'").

*Compression (up to* Compression-ct.64*)*

- Fixes [file service](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FGZipReadStream%20class%3E%3E%23fileReaderServicesForFile%3Asuffix%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://GZipReadStream class>>#fileReaderServicesForFile:suffix:") for installing compressed change sets.

*Network (up to* Network-nice.289/NetworkTests-pre.80*)*

- Improves [**new network support for IPv6**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20NetNameResolver%20getter%3A%20%23enableIPv6%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: NetNameResolver getter: #enableIPv6) open") and enables it by default. Extends object inspector and explorer representations of [sockets and socket addresses](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSocket%20newTCP%20explore%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Socket newTCP explore").
- Adds [file services](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20self%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27MailMessage%20class%20serviceViewMessage%27%27%20%27%27MailMessage%20class%20serviceExploreMessage%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27File%20services%20for%20mail%20messages%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('MailMessage class serviceViewMessage' 'MailMessage class serviceExploreMessage') name: 'File services for mail messages'") for viewing and exploring EML files as mail messages.
- Improves [**rendering of mail messages**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27MailMessage%20bodyTextFormatted%3AshowAttachments%3A%27%27%20%27%27MIMEDocument%20bodyTextFormatted%3AshowAttachments%3A%27%27%20%27%27MIMEDocumentTest%20testBodyTextFormatted%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Rendering%20of%20mail%20messages%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('MailMessage bodyTextFormatted:showAttachments:' 'MIMEDocument bodyTextFormatted:showAttachments:' 'MIMEDocumentTest testBodyTextFormatted') name: 'Rendering of mail messages'") that contain rich text or [attachments](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextMessageLink%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextMessageLink") and adds support for [inline (cid) images](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27MIMEContentRequest%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'MIMEContentRequest Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new").
- Extends [**SMTP client**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27SMTPClient%20Hierarchy%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'SMTPClient Hierarchy'; actOnClickFor: Model new") with new commands (verify, quit) and features (CRLF injection detection, 8BITMIME support, proper handling of 421 responses).
