- [Improves overall robustness and RFC compliance](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%7C%20text%20matcher%20tool%20%7C%5Crtext%20%3A%3D%20%28%28HtmlReadWriter%20on%3A%20%27%27Network-ct.263%3A%20Fixes%20a%20slip%20in%20mail%20composition%20that%20produced%20invalid%20recipient%20addresses%20according%20to%20RFC-5321.%20Addresses%20must%20not%20contain%20leading%20or%20trailing%20spaces.%20Some%20SMTP%20servers%20%28such%20as%20Gmail%29%20refuse%20to%20send%20these%20message.%5Cr%5CrNetwork-ct.268%2FNetworkTests-ct.65%3A%20Fixes%20parsing%20of%20multipart%20documents.%20As%20per%20RFC1341%2C%20the%20final%20separator%20can%20-%20or%20even%20should%20-%20be%20extended%20by%20two%20dashes.%5Cr%5CrNetwork-ct.269%3A%20Adds%20support%20for%20ascdate%2Fcdate%20format%20in%20MailMessage%20headers.%20While%20this%20is%20not%20covered%20by%20RFC822%2C%20some%20mboxes%20do%20contain%20this%20format%2C%20such%20as%20the%20older%20years%20of%20http%3A%2F%2Flists.squeakfoundation.org%2Fpipermail%2Fsqueak-dev%2F.%20Also%20fixes%20offset%20of%20fallback%20time.%5Cr%5CrNetwork-ct.275%3A%20Fixes%20serialization%20of%20base64-encoded%20documents.%5Cr%5CrNetwork-mt.276%2FNetworkTests-mt.70%3A%20Fixes%20parsing%20of%20MIMEType%20parameters.%5Cr%5CrNetwork-ct.279%2FNetworkTests-ct.74%3A%20Increases%20robustness%20of%20serializing%20incomplete%20MIMEDocuments.%20Some%20messages%20on%20squeak-dev%20contain%20incomplete%20multipart%20documents.%21%5Cr%5Dstyle%5B%2815%2017%2016%20186%2034%207%207%20112%2015%2018%2020%20125%2055%2023%2013%203%2015%207%2013%2031%2034%207%207%2026%2034%2025%2011%2094%29b%2C%2CRcode%3A%2F%2FMailComposition%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23doSendMail%3B%2C%2Cb%2C%2CRcode%3A%2F%2FMIMEDocument%20class%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23parseParts%3AwithSeparator%3A%3B%2C%2Cb%2C%2CRcode%3A%2F%2FMailMessage%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23timeFrom%3A%3B%2C%2CRhttp%3A%2F%2Flists.squeakfoundation.org%2Fpipermail%2Fsqueak-dev%2F%3B%2C%2CRcode%3A%2F%2FMailMessage%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23time%3B%2C%2Cb%2C%2CRcode%3A%2F%2FMIMEDocument%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23asSendableBodyText%3B%2C%2Cb%2C%2CRcode%3A%2F%2FMIMEType%20class%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23fromMIMEString%3A%3B%2C%2Cb%2C%2CRcode%3A%2F%2FMIMEDocument%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23asSendableBodyText%3B%2C%21%27%27%20readStream%29%20breakLines%3A%20false%3B%20nextText%29%20asString%20readStream%20nextChunkText.%5Cr%28matcher%20%3A%3D%20%27%27%28Network%28Tests%29%3F-%5Ba-z%5D%2B%5C%5C.%5C%5Cd%2B%29%27%27%20asRegex%29%20matchesIn%3A%20text%20do%3A%20%5B%3Amatch%20%7C%5Cr%20text%20addAttribute%3A%20%28PluggableTextAttribute%20evalBlock%3A%20%5B%28MCRepository%20trunk%20versionNamed%3A%20match%20asString%20%2C%20%27%27.mcz%27%27%29%20open%5D%29%20from%3A%20%28matcher%20subBeginning%3A%201%29%20first%20%2B%201%20to%3A%20%28matcher%20subEnd%3A%201%29%20first%5D.%5Crtool%20%3A%3D%20ToolBuilder%20open%3A%20%28Workspace%20new%20contents%3A%20text%29%20label%3A%20%27%27MIME%20update%20log%27%27.%5CrToolBuilder%20default%20class%20setBoundsForWindow%3A%20tool%20to%3A%20%28%28ToolBuilder%20default%20class%20getBoundsForWindow%3A%20tool%29%20origin%20extent%3A%20375px%40475px%29%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// | text matcher tool | text := ((HtmlReadWriter on: 'Network-ct.263: Fixes a slip in mail composition that produced invalid recipient addresses according to RFC-5321. Addresses must not contain leading or trailing spaces. Some SMTP servers (such as Gmail) refuse to send these message.  Network-ct.268/NetworkTests-ct.65: Fixes parsing of multipart documents. As per RFC1341, the final separator can - or even should - be extended by two dashes.  Network-ct.269: Adds support for ascdate/cdate format in MailMessage headers. While this is not covered by RFC822, some mboxes do contain this format, such as the older years of http://lists.squeakfoundation.org/pipermail/squeak-dev/. Also fixes offset of fallback time.  Network-ct.275: Fixes serialization of base64-encoded documents.  Network-mt.276/NetworkTests-mt.70: Fixes parsing of MIMEType parameters.  Network-ct.279/NetworkTests-ct.74: Increases robustness of serializing incomplete MIMEDocuments. Some messages on squeak-dev contain incomplete multipart documents.! ]style[(15 17 16 186 34 7 7 112 15 18 20 125 55 23 13 3 15 7 13 31 34 7 7 26 34 25 11 94)b,,Rcode://MailComposition&gt;&gt;#doSendMail;,,b,,Rcode://MIMEDocument class&gt;&gt;#parseParts:withSeparator:;,,b,,Rcode://MailMessage&gt;&gt;#timeFrom:;,,Rhttp://lists.squeakfoundation.org/pipermail/squeak-dev/;,,Rcode://MailMessage&gt;&gt;#time;,,b,,Rcode://MIMEDocument&gt;&gt;#asSendableBodyText;,,b,,Rcode://MIMEType class&gt;&gt;#fromMIMEString:;,,b,,Rcode://MIMEDocument&gt;&gt;#asSendableBodyText;,!' readStream) breakLines: false; nextText) asString readStream nextChunkText. (matcher := '(Network(Tests)?-[a-z]+\.\d+)' asRegex) matchesIn: text do: [:match |  text addAttribute: (PluggableTextAttribute evalBlock: [(MCRepository trunk versionNamed: match asString , '.mcz') open]) from: (matcher subBeginning: 1) first + 1 to: (matcher subEnd: 1) first]. tool := ToolBuilder open: (Workspace new contents: text) label: 'MIME update log'. ToolBuilder default class setBoundsForWindow: tool to: ((ToolBuilder default class getBoundsForWindow: tool) origin extent: 375px@475px)") (click for details!) of serializing and parsing MIME documents and mail messages.
- Implements case-insensitive comparison of [MIME types](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMIMEType%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MIMEType") and case-insensitive [access](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMIMEType%3E%3E%23parameterAt%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MIMEType>>#parameterAt:") to parameters.
- Improves robustness of [external-semaphore management](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSocket%3E%3E%23initializeSocketHandleUsing%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Socket>>#initializeSocketHandleUsing:") for sockets.
- Miscellaneous improvements to test coverage for MIME documents, MIME types, SMTP sending, and IPv6 sockets.
- Extends documentation for [MIMEType](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27MIMEType%20Comment%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'MIMEType Comment'; actOnClickFor: Model new") and [socket utilities](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSocket%20class%3E%3E%23ping%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Socket class>>#ping:") and [examples](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSocket%20class%3E%3E%23ping%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Socket class>>#ping:").
- Improves performance of [socket streaming](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSocketStream%3E%3E%23next%3Ainto%3AstartingAt%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SocketStream>>#next:into:startingAt:").

*SqueakSSL (up to* SqueakSSL-Core-mt.36*/*SqueakSSL-Tests-ct.30*)*

- Updates [list of current known bugs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakSSLTest%3E%3E%23expectedFailures%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakSSLTest>>#expectedFailures") for SqueakSSL on Linux and macOS.

*WebClient (up to* WebClient-Core-ul.138/WebClient-Tests-nice.66*)*

- Allows web servers to [process](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWebServer%3E%3E%23addService%3Aaction%3Amethods%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WebServer>>#addService:action:methods:") OPTIONS requests manually. For instance, this is required to handle CORS preflight requests properly.
- Several improvements and updates to [WebServer documentation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27WebServerHelp%20Help%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'WebServerHelp Help'; actOnClickFor: Model new").
- Sunsets JSON parser in [WebUtils](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27WebUtils%20class%20Definition%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'WebUtils class Definition'; actOnClickFor: Model new") in favor of the [JSON package](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FJson%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Json").
- Improves robustness of [chunked transfer decoding](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWebMessage%3E%3E%23chunkFrom%3Ato%3Aprogress%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WebMessage>>#chunkFrom:to:progress:") and fixes parsing of [WebCookie expiries](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWebCookie%3E%3E%23readExpiryFrom%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WebCookie>>#readExpiryFrom:").
- Speeds up [MD5 computation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWebUtils%20class%3E%3E%23md5Digest%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WebUtils class>>#md5Digest:") in WebUtils by up to 600%.

*JSON (up to* JSON-ul.68*)*

- Adds new [utility](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FCollection%3E%3E%23jsonAtPath%3AifLost%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Collection>>#jsonAtPath:ifLost:") for navigating JSON-ish objects along paths.
- Miscellaneous fixes for parsing [excessive inputs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FJson%3E%3E%23readFromString%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Json>>#readFromString:") or [negative zeros](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FJsonNumberParser%3E%3E%23nextNumber%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://JsonNumberParser>>#nextNumber:"), testing [key membership](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FOrderedJsonObject%3E%3E%23respondsTo%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://OrderedJsonObject>>#respondsTo:") of ordered JSON objects, [#at:ifAbsentPut:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23at%3AifAbsentPut%3A%20localToPackage%3A%20Json%20packageInfo%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #at:ifAbsentPut: localToPackage: Json packageInfo"), and rendering JSON objects [with non-string keys](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FJsonTests%3E%3E%23testWriteNonStringKey%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://JsonTests>>#testWriteNonStringKey").

*XML (up to* XML-Explorer-eem.3*)*

- Fixes a [performance issue](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20XMLElement%3E%3E%23asExplorerString%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: XMLElement>>#asExplorerString") when exploring huge XML documents.

**~~~ System Services ~~~**

*System (up to* System-ct.1522*)*

- **Introduces** [**ephemerons**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FEphemeron%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Ephemeron"), which support **instance-based** [**finalization**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FFinalizationRegistry%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://FinalizationRegistry"), a major improvement over the post-mortem finalization provided by WeakArrays. By using [ephemeron identity dictionaries](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FEphemeronIdentityDictionary%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://EphemeronIdentityDictionary") instead of [weak identity dictionaries](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FWeakIdentityKeyDictionary%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://WeakIdentityKeyDictionary"), weak indirect cyclic dependencies can now be properly garbage-collected for the first time.
- **Revises** [**extended clipboard interfaces**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseClassCommentOf%3A%20ExtendedClipboardInterface%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseClassCommentOf: ExtendedClipboardInterface") and enables them [by default](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FClipboard%3E%3E%23clipboardText%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Clipboard>>#clipboardText"). With this change, [pasting rich text from the host system](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%27%27%3Cb%3EDo%20this%3A%3C%2Fb%3E%20Switch%20to%20another%20application%20in%20your%20operating%20system%20such%20as%20web%20browser%20or%20a%20rich-text%20editor%20and%20copy%20some%20formatted%20text%20from%20it%20into%20the%20clipboard.%20Then%20come%20back%20and%20paste%20it%20here%21%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%3Cbr%3E%5Cr%27%27%20asTextFromHtml%20edit%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://'<b>Do this:</b> Switch to another application in your operating system such as web browser or a rich-text editor and copy some formatted text from it into the clipboard. Then come back and paste it here!<br> <br> ' asTextFromHtml edit") is supported in all operating systems. [Copying rich text](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FExtendedClipboardInterface%3E%3E%23clipboardText%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ExtendedClipboardInterface>>#clipboardText:") and [copying](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20ExtendedClipboardInterface%20category%3A%20%27%27general-api-add%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseClass: ExtendedClipboardInterface category: 'general-api-add'")/[pasting](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseClass%3A%20ExtendedClipboardInterface%20category%3A%20%27%27general-api-read%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseClass: ExtendedClipboardInterface category: 'general-api-read'") other formats such as pictures is still experimental.
- Improves [**change recovery**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27Recover%20changes%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'Recover changes'") by discovering class definitions and method removals in change records and parsing the selectors of do-its.
- **System navigation:**
  
  - Adds queries for browsing [methods with primitives](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openBrowser%3A%20%28SystemBrowser%20default%20fullOnClass%3A%20SystemNavigation%29%20filterMessages%3A%20%27%27primitive%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openBrowser: (SystemBrowser default fullOnClass: SystemNavigation) filterMessages: 'primitive'"), [pragmas in a package](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSystemNavigation%3E%3E%23allPragmaSelectorsLocalToPackage%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SystemNavigation>>#allPragmaSelectorsLocalToPackage:"), and methods or [classes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20SystemOrganizer%3E%3E%23superclassOrder%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: SystemOrganizer>>#superclassOrder:") in categories containing a [token](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSystemNavigation%3E%3E%23browseAllMethodsInCategoryToken%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SystemNavigation>>#browseAllMethodsInCategoryToken:").
  - Fixes queries for [callers in package](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20SystemNavigation%3E%3E%23allCallsOn%3Aand%3AlocalToPackage%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: SystemNavigation>>#allCallsOn:and:localToPackage:"), methods containing a [string](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20SystemNavigation%3E%3E%23allMethodsWithString%3AmatchCase%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: SystemNavigation>>#allMethodsWithString:matchCase:"), and methods containing [pool variables](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20SystemNavigation%3E%3E%23browseVariableReferences%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: SystemNavigation>>#browseVariableReferences:"), [unbound globals](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSystemNavigation%3E%3E%23methodsWithUnboundGlobals%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SystemNavigation>>#methodsWithUnboundGlobals"), or [unused blocks](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSystemNavigation%3E%3E%23unusedBlocks%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SystemNavigation>>#unusedBlocks").
- Adds facilities for enumerating the [class names in a system category](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSystemOrganizer%3E%3E%23classNamesIn%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SystemOrganizer>>#classNamesIn:") and the [versions of a class comment](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FChangeSet%20class%3E%3E%23scanClassCommentVersionsOf%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ChangeSet class>>#scanClassCommentVersionsOf:").
- Adds convenient accessors for [image header flags](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27SmalltalkImage%20imageHeaderFlagAt%3A%27%27%20%27%27SmalltalkImage%20imageHeaderFlagAt%3Aput%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Image%20header%20flag%20accessors%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('SmalltalkImage imageHeaderFlagAt:' 'SmalltalkImage imageHeaderFlagAt:put:') name: 'Image header flag accessors'") such as [high-DPI support](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSmalltalkImage%3E%3E%23upscaleDisplayOnHighDPI%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SmalltalkImage>>#upscaleDisplayOnHighDPI") and [threaded VMs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27SmalltalkImage%20processHasOSErrorInstVar%27%27%20%27%27SmalltalkImage%20processHasThreadAffinityInstVar%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Image%20header%20flags%20for%20threaded%20VMs%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('SmalltalkImage processHasOSErrorInstVar' 'SmalltalkImage processHasThreadAffinityInstVar') name: 'Image header flags for threaded VMs'"). Facilitates responding to platform changes after VM [restarts](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FBehavior%3E%3E%23startUp%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Behavior>>#startUp:") by [remembering](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSmalltalkImage%3E%3E%23previousPlatformName%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SmalltalkImage>>#previousPlatformName") the previous platform name. Improves [detection](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSmalltalkImage%3E%3E%23isHeadless%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SmalltalkImage>>#isHeadless") of headless mode.
- Adds support for [plain-text diffing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextDiffBuilder%3E%3E%23buildStringPatch%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextDiffBuilder>>#buildStringPatch") to complement existing [rich-text diffing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextDiffBuilder%3E%3E%23buildDisplayPatch%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextDiffBuilder>>#buildDisplayPatch").
- Adds an optional preference for [reusing the size of tool windows](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28Preferences%20pragmaPreferenceFor%3A%20RealEstateAgent%20getter%3A%20%23rememberWindowExtent%29%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(Preferences pragmaPreferenceFor: RealEstateAgent getter: #rememberWindowExtent) open"), which previously was available for debuggers only.
- For the [emergency mode REPL](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProject%20tryEmergencyEvaluatorForRecovery%3A%20%27%27This%20is%20a%20dummy%20error.%27%27.%20Display%20restore.%5Crself%20flag%3A%20%23doNotTest.%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Project tryEmergencyEvaluatorForRecovery: 'This is a dummy error.'. Display restore. self flag: #doNotTest."), adds a `clear` command and improves high-DPI support.
- Miscellaneous UI improvements:
  
  - Adds syntax highlighting when [editing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FChangeSetBrowser%20openOnCurrent%20offerWindowMenu.%5Cr%28Project%20current%20world%20findA%3A%20MenuMorph%29%20ifNotNil%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20checkedMatchString%3A%20%27%27add%27%27%5D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ChangeSetBrowser openOnCurrent offerWindowMenu. (Project current world findA: MenuMorph) ifNotNil: [:m | m checkedMatchString: 'add']") the preamble or postscript of a change set.
  - Revises the layout of the [annotation setup dialog](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27annotation%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'annotation'").
  - Automatically derives the [full name of known authors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUtilities%20class%3E%3E%23authorName%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Utilities class>>#authorName") from their initials.
  - Revises [algorithm for collapsing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20RealEstateAgent%20class%3E%3E%23assignCollapsePointFor%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: RealEstateAgent class>>#assignCollapsePointFor:") more windows than fit in one column on the screen.
  - For projects, adds an accessor to set an [explicit name](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FProject%3E%3E%23explicitName%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Project>>#explicitName:").
  - Improves UI strings and multilingual support in several places.
  - Tunes syntax highlighting colors for the [Community Dark](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27theme%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'theme'") and [Trim](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Extras%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27theme%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Extras'] filterItem: 'theme'") themes.
- [Refactors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloDiffFrom%3A%20%27%27System-mt.1381%27%27%20to%3A%20%27%27System-mt.1384%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloDiffFrom: 'System-mt.1381' to: 'System-mt.1384'") debugger invocation and resumption for different GUI frameworks, making it easier to write and integrate a custom debugger. Introduces a [debugger invocation cue](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDebuggerInvocationCue%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DebuggerInvocationCue") for passing along additional context such as an exception to the debugger.
- Refactors and fixes the [low-space watcher](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseClassCommentOf%3A%20LowSpaceWatcher%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseClassCommentOf: LowSpaceWatcher") for Spur VMs and tunes thresholds to avoid premature redundant warnings.
- Miscellaneous fixes:
  
  - Makes [saving projects](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Projects%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27save%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Projects'] filterItem: 'save'") more reliable and improves backward compatibility for [loading projects](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Projects%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27load%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Projects'] filterItem: 'load'")/[morphs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMorph%20class%3E%3E%23serviceLoadMorphFromFile%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Morph class>>#serviceLoadMorphFromFile") from older image versions.
  - For change files, improves multi-environment during [fileIn](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FChangeRecord%3E%3E%23fileIn%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ChangeRecord>>#fileIn")/[Out](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSystemOrganizer%3E%3E%23fileOut%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SystemOrganizer>>#fileOut"), browsing of [file-out classes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPseudoClass%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PseudoClass"), checking removed classes in a change set for [slips](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FChangeSet%3E%3E%23checkForSlips%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ChangeSet>>#checkForSlips"), and [condensing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSmalltalkImage%3E%3E%23condenseSources%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SmalltalkImage>>#condenseSources") into new `.sources` files.
  - Makes canceling [default app](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAppRegistry%20class%3E%3E%23default%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AppRegistry class>>#default") dialogs safer and allows providing empty credentials for mail senders.
  - Improves high-DPI support for window placement.
  - Improves headless support for [print-its](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDoItFirst%3E%3E%23doIt%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DoItFirst>>#doIt:"), [syntax errors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20CodeLoader%3E%3E%23installSourceFile%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: CodeLoader>>#installSourceFile:"), and [interactive dialogs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSmalltalkImage%3E%3E%23run%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SmalltalkImage>>#run:").
- Noteworthy optimizations for [user interface theme requests (speeds up typical tool building by ~50%)](https://lists.squeakfoundation.org/archives/list/squeak-dev%40lists.squeakfoundation.org/thread/Y2PRZLFEWSFKATITDM7PP2A6WB5XEG7Z/), [window collapsing](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Windows%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27collapse%20all%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Windows'] filterItem: 'collapse all'"), [changes enumeration](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20%28%5Bm%20wordingSelector%5D%20onDNU%3A%20%23wordingSelector%20do%3A%20%5Bnil%5D%29%20%3D%20%23browseChangesLabel%5D%20filterItem%3A%20nil%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | ([m wordingSelector] onDNU: #wordingSelector do: [nil]) = #browseChangesLabel] filterItem: nil"), and [space tallies](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSpaceTally%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SpaceTally").
- Improves documentation of several interfaces such as [system navigation queries](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSystemNavigation%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SystemNavigation"), [VM parameters](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSmalltalkImage%3E%3E%23vmParameterAt%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SmalltalkImage>>#vmParameterAt:"), and others.
- Cleans up unused preferences and project parameters.

*Regex (up to* Regex-Core-ct.93/Regex-Tests-Core-ct.38*)*

- Adds **new support** for [Unicode literals](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27RxParserTest%20testCodePointu%27%27%20%27%27RxParserTest%20testCodePointx%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Regex%20Unicode%20literals%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('RxParserTest testCodePointu' 'RxParserTest testCodePointx') name: 'Regex Unicode literals'") (`\x41`, `\u{1F388}`) and [character class escapes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27RxParserTest%20testCharacterSetWithEscapedCharacters%27%27%20%27%27RxParserTest%20testUnicodeCategory%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27Character%20class%20escapes%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('RxParserTest testCharacterSetWithEscapedCharacters' 'RxParserTest testUnicodeCategory') name: 'Character class escapes'") (`\p{N}, \P{Lu}`), [named capturing groups](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMessageNames%3A%20%27%27keyedSubexpression%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMessageNames: 'keyedSubexpression'") (`(?<num>\d+)/(?'den'\d+)`), and [non-capturing groups](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxMatcherTest%3E%3E%23testNonCapturingGroup%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxMatcherTest>>#testNonCapturingGroup") (`(?:ab)+`). Allows [nullable closures](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20%5Cr%20methodHierarchyBrowserForClass%3A%20RxsNode%5Cr%20selector%3A%20%23isNullable%20%5Cr%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation   methodHierarchyBrowserForClass: RxsNode  selector: #isNullable  ") to be repeated (`(a|b*)?`).
- Adds support for **leftmost-longest matching** (POSIX style) through an optional [flag](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxMatcher%3E%3E%23matchLongest%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxMatcher>>#matchLongest:").
- Adds [utility](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FString%3E%3E%23escapeForRegex%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://String>>#escapeForRegex") to escape strings for use in regular expressions.
- Adds [convenience selectors](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27RxMatcher%20allSubexpressions%27%27%20%27%27RxMatcher%20subexpressionRanges%3A%27%27%20%29%20name%3A%20%27%27New%20capture%20group%20accessors%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('RxMatcher allSubexpressions' 'RxMatcher subexpressionRanges:' ) name: 'New capture group accessors'") for accessing captured groups.
- Makes #asRegex [idempotent](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxMatcher%3E%3E%23asRegex%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxMatcher>>#asRegex") so methods that [expect](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FString%3E%3E%23occurrencesOfRegex%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://String>>#occurrencesOfRegex:") a pattern string can also take a preconfigured matcher instead.
- Fixes several issues with parsing [nested](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxParserTest%3E%3E%23testNestedQuantifiers%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxParserTest>>#testNestedQuantifiers") or [repeated](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxParserTest%3E%3E%23testQuantifierSequence%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxParserTest>>#testQuantifierSequence") quantifiers and [lookaround-alike](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxParserTest%3E%3E%23testLookaroundParser%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxParserTest>>#testLookaroundParser") patterns.
- Fixes incorrect order of [capture groups within branches](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxMatcherTest%3E%3E%23testCapturesWithinBranches%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxMatcherTest>>#testCapturesWithinBranches") and removes redundant [capturing of lookarounds](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FRxParserTest%3E%3E%23testNoCapturingOfLookarounds%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://RxParserTest>>#testNoCapturingOfLookarounds").
- Repairs broken [regex prompt dialog](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUIManager%3E%3E%23request%3Aregex%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UIManager>>#request:regex:").

*SystemReporter (up to* SystemReporter-eem.68*)*

- Updates [description](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openSystemReporterOnVMParameters%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openSystemReporterOnVMParameters") for Cog VM parameters and image-header flags.

*PreferenceBrowser (*up to PreferenceBrowser-codefrau.159*)*

- Improves robustness of preference wizard when adjusting settings for low performance.

*Additional packages:*

- [**FFI**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPreferenceWizardMorph%3E%3E%23installFFI%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PreferenceWizardMorph>>#installFFI")**:**
  
  - A [threaded version](https://doi.org/10.1145/3660829.3660846) of the OpenSmalltalk VM that can run primitives and FFI callouts in the background is under development.
  - Improves FFI exception handling by offering a [configurable exception handling mode](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20requireFFI%3A%20%5B%28Smalltalk%20at%3A%20%23ExternalFunction%29%20class%3E%3E%23setFFIExceptionHandlingMode%3A%5D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes requireFFI: [(Smalltalk at: #ExternalFunction) class>>#setFFIExceptionHandlingMode:]"), adding support for PrimitiveError and ExceptionInFFICallError for [failed FFI callouts](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20requireFFI%3A%20%5B%28Smalltalk%20at%3A%20%23ExternalFunction%29%20class%3E%3E%23externalCallFailedWith%3A%5D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes requireFFI: [(Smalltalk at: #ExternalFunction) class>>#externalCallFailedWith:]"), and adding a [new pragma for asynchronous error reporting](https://source.squeak.org/FFI/FFI-Kernel-eem.237.diff) in ThreadedFFI.
  - Adds [support for varargs](https://source.squeak.org/FFI/FFI-Kernel-eem.234.diff) (optional arguments) in C declarations.
  - Adds support for ARM64.
  - Several robustness improvements to the type framework, thread safety, and platform changes.
  - Adds more convenient [Win32 utils](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20requireFFI%3A%20%5Bself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOfList%3A%20%23%28getComputerName%20getCurrentUserDomain%29%5D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes requireFFI: [self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOfList: #(getComputerName getCurrentUserDomain)]").
  - Overall improvements to test coverage, documentation, and performance.
- [**OSProcess**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FPreferenceWizardMorph%3E%3E%23installOSProcess%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://PreferenceWizardMorph>>#installOSProcess")**:**
  
  - Enables buffered readers (such as PipeableOSProcess from companion package CommandShell) to stream over a very large input stream by implementing [automatic pruning](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%20requireOSProcess%3A%20%5B%28Smalltalk%20at%3A%20%23ResettableBufferStream%29%3E%3E%23pruneCollection%5D%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes requireOSProcess: [(Smalltalk at: #ResettableBufferStream)>>#pruneCollection]").
  - Adds support for Cuis and Unicode strings.
  - Sunsets support for the [IOHandle](https://wiki.squeak.org/squeak/996) package, which is no longer maintained for recent versions of Squeak.
  - Overall stability and performance improvements.
- [Refactoring tools](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20PreferenceWizardMorph%3E%3E%23installRefactoringTools%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// PreferenceWizardMorph>>#installRefactoringTools"): Minor bug fixes, extensions, and performance improvements for refactoring rules.
- A new version of [**Squot**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20SqueakReleaseNotes%5Cr%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Tools%27%27%5D%5Cr%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27Git%20Browser%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// SqueakReleaseNotes  openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Tools']  filterItem: 'Git Browser'") was [released](https://github.com/hpi-swa/Squot/releases), which adds support for `git revert`, tags, and co-authors, contains several UI improvements, and improves overall stability and performance.
- The first stable version of [**Squeak Inbox Talk**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28HelpBrowser%20openOn%3A%20SqueakContributionHelp%29%20model%20showTopicNamed%3A%20%23squeakInboxTalk%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// (HelpBrowser openOn: SqueakContributionHelp) model showTopicNamed: #squeakInboxTalk") was [released](https://github.com/hpi-swa-lab/squeak-inbox-talk/releases/tag/v1.0), which integrates the new HyperKitty archives for the Squeak mailing lists, adds support for rich-text messages and attachments (only available in the Squeak Trunk version), and contains several UI improvements.

**~~~ Version Control & Package Management ~~~**

*Installer (VersionNumber, UpdateStream, Squeak-Version) (up to* Installer-Core-ct.458/VersionNumber-eem.5*)*

- Adds support for [HTTPS schemes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27InstallerMonticello%20https%3A%27%27%20%27%27InstallerMonticello%20https%3Auser%3Apassword%3A%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27HTTPs%20scheme%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('InstallerMonticello https:' 'InstallerMonticello https:user:password:') name: 'HTTPs scheme'") to [InstallerMonticello](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTextLink%20new%20classAndMethod%3A%20%27%27InstallerMonticello%20Definition%27%27%3B%20actOnClickFor%3A%20Model%20new%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TextLink new classAndMethod: 'InstallerMonticello Definition'; actOnClickFor: Model new"). Uses HTTPS for [source.squeak.org](https://source.squeak.org) and [squeaksource.com](https://squeaksource.com).
- [Avoids](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMetacelloStub%20class%3E%3E%23apiSelectors%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MetacelloStub class>>#apiSelectors") compiler and linter warnings when installing a Metacello package for the first time.
- Fixes a bug that polluted the current change set during the [installation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FInstaller%20class%3E%3E%23ensureRecentMetacello%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://Installer class>>#ensureRecentMetacello") of Metacello. Slightly accelerates installation.
- Fixes [comparison](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FVersionNumber%3E%3E%23%3C%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://VersionNumber>>#<") of version numbers with different lengths.
- Fixes multi-environment issue when [unloading](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%23unloadCleanUp%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://#unloadCleanUp") packages via Monticello.

*Monticello (up to* Monticello-ct.822/MonticelloConfigurations-codefrau.187*)*

- **Adds "file out theirs"** to the patch list menu of code dialogs (such as when saving a version or [comparing two snapshots](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleMonticelloDiff%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleMonticelloDiff")).
- Makes **"add (all) to current change set"** in code dialogs capable of class and script definitions.
- Improves support for HTTPS repositories and enables [migration](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMCMcmUpdater%20class%3E%3E%23alternativeDescriptionOf%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MCMcmUpdater class>>#alternativeDescriptionOf:") of existing HTTP repositories to HTTPS.
- Adds utility for [eradicating](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMCWorkingCopyBrowser%3E%3E%23eradicateRepository%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MCWorkingCopyBrowser>>#eradicateRepository") a repository from all working copies.
- Improves robustness of [MCFileBasedRepository&gt;&gt;versionNamed:](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMCFileBasedRepository%3E%3E%23versionNamed%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MCFileBasedRepository>>#versionNamed:") by automatically appending a missing MCZ extension.
- Adds, improves, and fixes [several dialogs](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%20%27%27-%20When%20%3Ca%20href%3D%5C%22code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleMonticelloConfiguration%5C%22%3Ebrowsing%20configurations%3C%2Fa%3E%20in%20a%20repository%2C%20displays%20their%20metadata%20in%20the%20bottom%20pane.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr-%20When%20%3Ca%20href%3D%5C%22code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20exampleMonticelloVersion%5C%22%3Ebrowsing%20versions%3C%2Fa%3E%2C%20also%20displays%20the%20full%20name%20of%20the%20author.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr-%20To%20the%20%3Ca%20href%3D%5C%22code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20%28m%20owner%20submorphIndexOf%3A%20m%29%20%3D%201%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%26apos%3Bupdate%20squeak%26apos%3B%5C%22%3E%26quot%3Bupdate%20completed%26quot%3B%20dialog%3C%2Fa%3E%2C%20adds%20a%20short%20reminder%20when%20a%20newer%20version%20of%20the%20OpenSmalltalk%20VM%20is%20available.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr-%20Makes%20%26quot%3Bupdate%20completed%26quot%3B%20dialog%20robust%20against%20unavailable%20CI%20information.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr-%20%3Ca%20href%3D%5C%22code%3A%2F%2FMCRepository%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23copyImageVersions%5C%22%3EPrevents%3C%2Fa%3E%20accidental%20bulk%20copying%20of%20versions%20to%20trunk%20repositories.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr-%20Improves%20confirmation%20message%20for%20%26quot%3Bcopy%20image%20versions%20here%26quot%3B%20from%20%3Ca%20href%3D%5C%22code%3A%2F%2FMCWorkingCopyBrowser%20open.%20nil%5C%22%3Eworking%20copy%20browsers%3C%2Fa%3E.%3Cbr%3E%5Cr-%20Polishes%20and%20fixes%20%3Ca%20href%3D%5C%22code%3A%2F%2FMCWorkingCopyBrowser%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23saveVersion%5C%22%3Eseveral%3C%2Fa%3E%20%3Ca%20href%3D%5C%22code%3A%2F%2FMCWorkingCopyBrowser%26gt%3B%26gt%3B%23flushCachedVersions%5C%22%3Eother%3C%2Fa%3E%20dialogs.%27%27%20asTextFromHtml%20edit%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://  '- When <a href=\"code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleMonticelloConfiguration\">browsing configurations</a> in a repository, displays their metadata in the bottom pane.<br> - When <a href=\"code://SqueakReleaseNotes exampleMonticelloVersion\">browsing versions</a>, also displays the full name of the author.<br> - To the <a href=\"code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | (m owner submorphIndexOf: m) = 1] filterItem: &apos;update squeak&apos;\">&quot;update completed&quot; dialog</a>, adds a short reminder when a newer version of the OpenSmalltalk VM is available.<br> - Makes &quot;update completed&quot; dialog robust against unavailable CI information.<br> - <a href=\"code://MCRepository&gt;&gt;#copyImageVersions\">Prevents</a> accidental bulk copying of versions to trunk repositories.<br> - Improves confirmation message for &quot;copy image versions here&quot; from <a href=\"code://MCWorkingCopyBrowser open. nil\">working copy browsers</a>.<br> - Polishes and fixes <a href=\"code://MCWorkingCopyBrowser&gt;&gt;#saveVersion\">several</a> <a href=\"code://MCWorkingCopyBrowser&gt;&gt;#flushCachedVersions\">other</a> dialogs.' asTextFromHtml edit").
- Fixes loading of classes and trait definitions [without comments](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Monticello-mad.784%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Monticello-mad.784'").
- Fixes [multi-environment issues](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Monticello-ct.820%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Monticello-ct.820'") when loading/unloading organization definitions.
- Fixes [merging](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Monticello-eem.811%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Monticello-eem.811'") and ["copy reference"](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMCCodeTool%3E%3E%23copyReference%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MCCodeTool>>#copyReference") of removed definitions.
- Improves handling of [#acceptsLoggingOfCompilation](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseAllImplementorsOf%3A%20%23acceptsLoggingOfCompilation%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseAllImplementorsOf: #acceptsLoggingOfCompilation") while loading packages.
- Fixes [change detection](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMCPackageManager%20class%3E%3E%23reregisterForNotificationsWith%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MCPackageManager class>>#reregisterForNotificationsWith:") of working copies when renaming categories.
- Improves [error reporting](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FMCHttpRepository%3E%3E%23webClientDo%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://MCHttpRepository>>#webClientDo:") when accessing HTTP repositories.

*SqueakMap (up to* SMBase-ct.147*/*SMLoader-ct.102*)*

- Fixes a bug when installing missing WebClient via SqueakMap.

**~~~ Help & Internationalization ~~~**

*GetText*

- No significant changes.

*Multilingual (up to* Multilingual-mt.293*)*

- Fixes a [bug](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openMonticelloVersion%3A%20%27%27Multilingual-mt.290%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openMonticelloVersion: 'Multilingual-mt.290'") in UTF16 conversion for applying line-end conventions in little endian.
- Improves documentation on [UTF8 encoding/decoding](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2Fself%20systemNavigation%20browseMessageList%3A%20%23%28%27%27ByteArray%20utf8Decoded%27%27%20%27%27String%20utf8Encoded%27%27%29%20name%3A%20%27%27UTF8%20encoding%2Fdecoding%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://self systemNavigation browseMessageList: #('ByteArray utf8Decoded' 'String utf8Encoded') name: 'UTF8 encoding/decoding'").

*Help (up to* HelpSystem-Core-ct.159/Help-Squeak-Project-ct.105*)*

- **Help contents:**
  
  - **Adds a tutorial on** [**how to contribute to Squeak**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20openDockingBarMenuThat%3A%20%5B%3Am%20%7C%20m%20contents%20%3D%20%27%27Help%27%27%5D%20filterItem%3A%20%27%27Contribut%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes openDockingBarMenuThat: [:m | m contents = 'Help'] filterItem: 'Contribut'")**.** Includes a short description of Squeak's community model, our development process, and recommended tools such as Monticello and Squeak Inbox Talk. It has never been easier to join the community - go ahead and make your first contribution NOW! :-)
  - **Updates** [**keyboard shortcut reference**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28HelpBrowser%20openOn%3A%20SqueakTutorialsCommandKey%29%20model%20showFirstTopic%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(HelpBrowser openOn: SqueakTutorialsCommandKey) model showFirstTopic") with global shortcuts and shortcuts for windows, menus, lists, and trees as well as instructions to customize shortcuts.
  - Improves and updates several sections of the [terse guide](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28HelpBrowser%20openOn%3A%20TerseGuideHelp%29%20model%20showFirstTopic%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(HelpBrowser openOn: TerseGuideHelp) model showFirstTopic").
  - Minor clean-ups and improvements on various help topics. Updates [collection of online resources](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28HelpBrowser%20openOn%3A%20SqueakProjectHelp%29%20model%20showTopicNamed%3A%20%23squeakResourcesOnline%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(HelpBrowser openOn: SqueakProjectHelp) model showTopicNamed: #squeakResourcesOnline").
- Moves release notes (like this one!) [into the image](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20class%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes class"), which were previously managed on GitHub. They are [mirrored](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FSqueakReleaseNotes%20class%3E%3E%23storeAsJekyllFilesInDirectoryNamed%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://SqueakReleaseNotes class>>#storeAsJekyllFilesInDirectoryNamed:") to [squeak.org/release\_notes/](https://squeak.org/release_notes/), so you can view them and click on examples to run them in SqueakJS even if you have no image at hand (how dare you!).
- **Help system:**
  
  - **Expands support for** [**editing help through the help browser**](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%28HelpBrowser%20openOn%3A%20HelpOnHelp%29%20model%20showTopicNamed%3A%20%23writingYourOwnHelp%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://(HelpBrowser openOn: HelpOnHelp) model showTopicNamed: #writingYourOwnHelp") **without writing code.** Users can now manage custom help topics and file-based help topics by using the context menu in the tree view or dragging and dropping topics.
  - Revises icons in help browsers for empty help books, references of generic classes, and search results.
  - Refactors [help search](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20SearchTopic%3E%3E%23startSearch%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: SearchTopic>>#startSearch") and integrations for [files/directories](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAbstractFileBasedHelpTopic%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AbstractFileBasedHelpTopic") and [HTML sites](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FHtmlHelpTopic%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://HtmlHelpTopic") into the help system. In [directory-based help topics](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FDirectoryBasedHelpTopic%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://DirectoryBasedHelpTopic"), now uses the same [sorting strategy](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FAbstractFileBasedHelpTopic%3E%3E%23sortBlock%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://AbstractFileBasedHelpTopic>>#sortBlock:") for child directories (recursively).
  - Stability improvements for browsing single styled topics, editing contents of directory topics, and searching topics.

**~~~ Deployment & Miscellaneous ~~~**

*Tests (up to* Tests-ct.522*)*

- There are now over [5800 tests](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FTestRunner%20open%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://TestRunner open") in this release that run in less than 3 minutes on our [CI](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%20%28%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20SystemVersion%20current%20ciStatusPageUrl%29%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%29%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// ((TextURL new url: SystemVersion current ciStatusPageUrl) actOnClickFor: nil)").
- Improves precision and coverage of tests for DataStreams, [UI themes](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FUserInterfaceThemeTest%3E%3E%23test01ImplementationHooks%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://UserInterfaceThemeTest>>#test01ImplementationHooks"), and [package dependencies](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2FToolSet%20browseMethodVersion%3A%20PackageDependencyTest%3E%3E%23testPackage%3AdependsExactlyOn%3A%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code://ToolSet browseMethodVersion: PackageDependencyTest>>#testPackage:dependsExactlyOn:").
- Improves safety of [several tests](https://try.squeak.org/#url=images&zip=Squeak6.1.zip&wizard=false&demo=true&navigateToReleaseNotes=true&argv=%5B%22squeakjs%22%2C%22squeak.image%22%2C%22--doit%22%2C%22%28TextURL%20new%20url%3A%20%27code%3A%2F%2F%5Cr%27%27Prevents%20CompilerExceptionsTest%20from%20touching%20the%20changes%20file.%5CrCleans%20up%20temporary%20files%20in%20SoundTest.%5CrCleans%20up%20temporary%20change%20sets%20in%20Monticello%20tests%20and%20avoids%20touching%20the%20current%20change%20set.%5CrFixes%20image%20segment%20tests%20to%20not%20damage%20existing%20classes%20in%20the%20image.%5CrDisables%20broken%20testForceChangesToDiskRobustness%20on%20Linux%20to%20prevent%20VM%20crashes.%5CrExtracts%20smoke%20tests%20for%20installer%2C%20which%20could%20previously%20load%20outdated%20versions%20of%20several%20packages%20into%20your%20image%2C%20into%20a%20separate%20class%20that%20is%20opt-in%20via%20InstallerSmokeTest%20class%3E%3E%23generateTests.%27%27%5Cr%20editWithLabel%3A%20%27%27Improved%20tests%27%27%27%29%20future%20actOnClickFor%3A%20nil%22%5D "code:// 'Prevents CompilerExceptionsTest from touching the changes file. Cleans up temporary files in SoundTest. Cleans up temporary change sets in Monticello tests and avoids touching the current change set. Fixes image segment tests to not damage existing classes in the image. Disables broken testForceChangesToDiskRobustness on Linux to prevent VM crashes. Extracts smoke tests for installer, which could previously load outdated versions of several packages into your image, into a separate class that is opt-in via InstallerSmokeTest class>>#generateTests.'  editWithLabel: 'Improved tests'") that might have corrupted or crashed the image before.

*ReleaseBuilder (up to* ReleaseBuilder-mt.248*)*

- Minor improvements for setting preferences reproducibly when creating new release images.

**Major Deprecations** (up to 60Deprecated-ct.117/61Deprecated-ct.29)
